    76	/** An envelope must itself be sound: right version, intact hash, a task its own menu allows. Builders assert this; the validator trusts only a sound one. */
    77	export function validateEnvelope(env: PredictionEnvelope): Validation {
    78	  const errors: string[] = [];
    79	  if (env.version !== ENVELOPE_VERSION) errors.push(`envelope version ${env.version}`);
    80	  if (!envelopeIntact(env)) errors.push("envelope hash does not match its content");
    81	  errors.push(...taskSchedule(env).errors);
    82	  return { ok: errors.length === 0, errors };
    83	}
    84	
    85	export function validatePrediction(p: unknown, env: PredictionEnvelope): Validation {
    86	  const errors: string[] = [];
    87	  if (!isPlain(p)) return { ok: false, errors: ["a prediction must be a plain JSON object (no inherited fields)"] };
    88	  for (const k of Object.keys(p)) {
    89	    if (FORBIDDEN.includes(k)) errors.push(`forbidden key ${JSON.stringify(k)}`);
    90	    else if (!PRED_KEYS.includes(k)) errors.push(`unknown key ${JSON.stringify(k)}`);
    91	  }
    92	  if (errors.length) return { ok: false, errors };
    93	  // Required fields must be the object's own: an inherited field would validate but serialize as absent.
    94	  for (const k of ["version", "caseId", "envelopeHash", "task", "abstain"]) if (!Object.hasOwn(p, k)) errors.push(`missing own field ${JSON.stringify(k)}`);
    95	  if (errors.length) return { ok: false, errors };
    96	  let stable = false;
    97	  try {
    98	    stable = canonical(p) === canonical(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(p)));
    99	  } catch {
   100	    stable = false;
   101	  }
   102	  if (!stable) return { ok: false, errors: ["the prediction does not survive JSON serialization unchanged"] };
   103	  if (p.version !== PREDICTION_VERSION) errors.push(`version must be ${PREDICTION_VERSION}`);
   104	  if (p.caseId !== env.caseId) errors.push(`caseId ${JSON.stringify(p.caseId)} is not ${JSON.stringify(env.caseId)}`);
   105	  if (p.envelopeHash !== env.envelopeHash) errors.push("envelopeHash does not match the envelope");
   106	  if (!isPlain(p.task)) errors.push("task is missing or not a plain object");
   107	  else {
   108	    let same = false;
   109	    try {
   110	      same = canonical(p.task) === canonical(env.task);
   111	    } catch {
   112	      same = false;
   113	    }
   114	    if (!same) errors.push("task does not restate the envelope's task verbatim (the question is fixed by the host)");
   115	  }
   116	  if (typeof p.abstain !== "boolean") errors.push("abstain must be a boolean");
   117	  if (p.reason !== undefined && typeof p.reason !== "string") errors.push("reason must be a string");
   118	  if (p.abstain === true) {
   119	    if (Object.hasOwn(p, "probs")) errors.push("an abstention carries no probs");
   120	  } else if (p.abstain === false) {
   121	    if (!isPlain(p.probs)) errors.push("probs are required unless abstaining, as a plain object");
   122	    else {
   123	      const pr = p.probs;
   124	      for (const k of Object.keys(pr)) if (!(CLASSES as readonly string[]).includes(k)) errors.push(`probs has unknown key ${JSON.stringify(k)}`);
   125	      let sum = 0;
   126	      for (const c of CLASSES) {
   127	        const v = Object.hasOwn(pr, c) ? pr[c] : undefined;
   128	        if (!Number.isInteger(v) || (v as number) < 0 || (v as number) > 1000) errors.push(`probs.${c} must be an integer in 0..1000 (per-mille)`);
   129	        else sum += v as number;
   130	      }
   131	      if (errors.length === 0 && sum !== 1000) errors.push(`probs must sum to exactly 1000, got ${sum}`);
   132	    }
   133	  }
   134	  return { ok: errors.length === 0, errors };
   135	}
   136	
   137	/** argmax of the stated probs, tie order none > down > up (derived, never submitted). */
   138	export function argmax(p: Probs): Direction3 {
   139	  let best: Direction3 = "none";
   140	  for (const c of ["none", "down", "up"] as const) if (p[c] > p[best]) best = c;
   141	  return best;
   142	}
   143	
   144	export interface FileCheck {
   145	  /** caseId -> prediction-or-null (null: invalid, or no valid line). */
   146	  byCase: Map<string, { prediction: Prediction | null; errors: string[] }>;
   147	  invalid: number;
   148	  missing: string[];
   149	  extra: string[];
   150	  duplicates: string[];
   151	  notObject: number;
   152	  /** Envelopes that fail validateEnvelope (version, recomputed hash, task on its own menu): their cases cannot be answered validly. */
   153	  badEnvelopes: string[];
   154	}
   155	
   156	/** File-level validation: exactly one line per case. Extras, duplicates and missing cases are counted; a duplicated case scores as invalid. */
   157	export function validatePredictionFile(lines: string[], envelopes: readonly PredictionEnvelope[]): FileCheck {
   158	  const byEnv = new Map(envelopes.map((e) => [e.caseId, e]));
   159	  const out: FileCheck = { byCase: new Map(), invalid: 0, missing: [], extra: [], duplicates: [], notObject: 0, badEnvelopes: [] };
   160	  const unsound = new Map<string, string[]>();
   161	  for (const e of envelopes) {
   162	    const v = validateEnvelope(e);
   163	    if (!v.ok) {
   164	      unsound.set(e.caseId, v.errors);
   165	      out.badEnvelopes.push(e.caseId);
   166	    }
   167	  }
   168	  const seen = new Set<string>();
   169	  for (const line of lines) {
   170	    if (line.trim() === "") continue;
   171	    let raw: unknown;
   172	    try {
   173	      raw = JSON.parse(line);
   174	    } catch {
   175	      out.notObject++;
   176	      out.invalid++;
   177	      continue;
   178	    }
   179	    if (!isPlain(raw)) {
   180	      out.notObject++;
   181	      out.invalid++;
   182	      continue;
   183	    }
   184	    const id = typeof raw.caseId === "string" ? raw.caseId : "";
   185	    const env = byEnv.get(id);
   186	    if (!env) {
   187	      out.extra.push(id);
   188	      out.invalid++;
   189	      continue;
   190	    }
   191	    if (seen.has(id)) {
   192	      out.duplicates.push(id);
   193	      out.byCase.set(id, { prediction: null, errors: ["duplicate line for this case"] });
   194	      out.invalid++;
   195	      continue;
   196	    }
   197	    seen.add(id);
   198	    const v = unsound.has(id) ? { ok: false, errors: ["the envelope is unsound: " + unsound.get(id)!.join("; ")] } : validatePrediction(raw, env);
   199	    if (!v.ok) out.invalid++;
   200	    out.byCase.set(id, { prediction: v.ok ? (raw as unknown as Prediction) : null, errors: v.errors });
   201	  }
   202	  for (const e of envelopes) if (!seen.has(e.caseId)) out.missing.push(e.caseId);
   203	  return out;
   204	}

     8	export const ENVELOPE_VERSION = 1;

    10	export const CLASSES = ["up", "down", "none"] as const;
    11	export type Direction3 = (typeof CLASSES)[number];
    12	export type OpKind = "lesion" | "feed" | "drain" | "none";

    14	/** One operation. A drain's `amount` is positive (nutrient taken per cell); the scheduled feed carries it negated. A sham has no other key. */
    15	export interface TaskOp {

    26	  delta: number;

    57	export interface PredictionEnvelope {

    64	  config: WorldConfig;

    66	  frames: Frame[];
    67	  menu: Menu;
    68	  task: Task;
    69	  envelopeHash: string;

    72	export const FRAME_SIDE = 8;
    73	
    74	export function standardMenu(cfg: WorldConfig, deltaMax: number, amountMax: number, rMax: number): Menu {
    75	  void cfg;
    76	  return {
    77	    ops: [
    78	      { kind: "lesion", rMin: 1, rMax },
    79	      { kind: "feed", rMin: 1, rMax, amountMin: 1, amountMax },
    80	      { kind: "drain", rMin: 1, rMax, amountMin: 1, amountMax },
    81	      { kind: "none" },
    82	    ],
    83	    deltaMin: 1,
    84	    deltaMax,
    85	  };
    86	}

    88	export function censusPoint(s: WorldState, living: number, free: number, mass: number): CensusPoint {
    89	  return { step: s.step, livingCells: living, freeNutrient: free, mass };
    90	}
    91	
    92	/** A frame sample copied out of a state: B+P mass per block. */
    93	export function frameOf(s: WorldState, side = FRAME_SIDE): Frame {
    94	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
    95	  const W = worldW(s.cfg);
    96	  const H = n / W;
    97	  const bw = Math.floor(W / side), bh = Math.floor(H / side);
    98	  const mass = new Array<number>(side * side).fill(0);
    99	  for (let y = 0; y < bh * side; y++) {
   100	    for (let x = 0; x < bw * side; x++) {
   101	      const i = y * W + x;
   102	      mass[Math.floor(y / bh) * side + Math.floor(x / bw)] += s.cells[CH.B * n + i] + s.cells[CH.P * n + i];
   103	    }
   104	  }
   105	  return { step: s.step, side, mass };
   106	}
   107	
   108	export function envelopeBody(e: PredictionEnvelope): Omit<PredictionEnvelope, "envelopeHash"> {
   109	  const { envelopeHash: _h, ...rest } = e;
   110	  void _h;
   111	  return rest;
   112	}
   113	export const hashEnvelopeBody = (b: Omit<PredictionEnvelope, "envelopeHash">): string => sha256Text(canonical(b));
   114	
   115	export function sealEnvelope(b: Omit<PredictionEnvelope, "envelopeHash">): PredictionEnvelope {
   116	  assertNoPostFork(b);
   117	  return { ...b, envelopeHash: hashEnvelopeBody(b) };
   118	}
   119	
   120	/** Throws unless every census point and frame is at a step <= the fork step. */
   121	export function assertNoPostFork(b: Omit<PredictionEnvelope, "envelopeHash">): void {
   122	  const C = b.task.forkStep;
   123	  const max = Math.max(-1, ...b.census.map((c) => c.step), ...b.frames.map((f) => f.step));
   124	  if (max > C) throw new Error(`envelope ${b.caseId} holds data at step ${max}, after its fork step ${C}`);
   125	}
   126	
   127	export function envelopeIntact(e: PredictionEnvelope): boolean {
   128	  return e.envelopeHash === hashEnvelopeBody(envelopeBody(e));
   129	}

    11	/** Entries in sorted key order: the markdown must not depend on whether the comparison came fresh from memory or back from canonical JSON (sorted keys). */
    12	const ent = <T>(o: Record<string, T>): [string, T][] => Object.entries(o).sort((a, b) => (a[0] < b[0] ? -1 : a[0] > b[0] ? 1 : 0));
    13	const f3 = (v: number | null): string => (v === null ? "n/a" : v.toFixed(3));

    15	function tableMd(t: SplitTable): string {
    16	  const lines = [`### ${t.split} (${t.nCases} cases, ${t.nFamilies} families; up ${t.classCounts.up}, down ${t.classCounts.down}, none ${t.classCounts.none})`, "", "| proposer | role | answered | abstained | invalid | missing | coverage | Brier (imputed) | Brier (answered) | direction acc. | balanced acc. | acc. at matched coverage | valid non-null yield |", "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|"];
    17	  for (const r of t.rows) lines.push(`| ${r.id} | ${r.role} | ${r.answered} | ${r.abstained} | ${r.invalid} | ${r.missing} | ${f3(r.coverage)} | ${f3(r.brierImputed)} | ${f3(r.brierAnswered)} | ${f3(r.directionalAccuracy)} | ${f3(r.balancedAccuracy)} | ${f3(r.accuracyAtMatchedCoverage)} | ${f3(r.validNonNullYield)} |`);
    18	  lines.push("", `Mean absolute effect: ${ent(t.meanAbsEffect).map(([k, v]) => `${k} ${v === null ? "n/a" : v.toFixed(1)}`).join("; ")}.`, "");
    19	  return lines.join("\n");
    20	}

    22	export function comparisonMarkdown(c: Comparison, minEffect: number): string {

    24	  out.push("## Family-level intervals (imputed Brier, whole-family bootstrap)", "");

    29	      for (const [id, iv] of ent(v.pairedVsStrongest)) if (iv) out.push(`  - ${id} minus ${c.strongestBaselineOnDev}: ${f3(iv.mean)} [${f3(iv.lo)}, ${f3(iv.hi)}]`);

    31	  }
    32	  out.push("", "## Mechanism audit (measured vs fixture expectation)", "", "| mechanism | expected | up | down | none |", "|---|---|---|---|---|");
    33	  for (const m of c.mechanismAudit) out.push(`| ${m.mechanism} | ${m.expected} | ${m.counts.up} | ${m.counts.down} | ${m.counts.none} |`);
    34	  out.push("", "## File-level diagnostics (per proposer, over the envelopes it was meant to answer)", "", "| proposer | lines | invalid | missing | extra | duplicates | not an object | unsound envelopes |", "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|");
    35	  for (const [id, d] of ent(c.fileDiagnostics)) out.push(`| ${id} | ${d.lines} | ${d.invalid} | ${d.missing} | ${d.extra} | ${d.duplicates} | ${d.notObject} | ${d.badEnvelopes} |`);
    36	  out.push("", "## Predicted class on the test split (answered cases)", "", "| proposer | up | down | none |", "|---|---|---|---|");
    37	  for (const [id, a] of ent(c.argmaxTest)) out.push(`| ${id} | ${a.up} | ${a.down} | ${a.none} |`);
    38	  const t = c.trendConfound;
    39	  out.push("", "## Trend-confound diagnostic", "", t.description, "", `- share of cases labelled up: paired rule ${f3(t.pairedAlwaysRisingAccuracy)}, naive before/after rule ${f3(t.naiveBeforeAfterAlwaysRisingAccuracy)}; cases with treated above the fork value: ${t.risingCases}/${t.totalCases}`, `- sham: ${t.sham.n} cases, naive rule says up on ${t.sham.naiveUp}, paired rule says none on ${t.sham.pairedNone}`, `- low-dose feed: ${t.lowDose.n} cases, naive rule says up on ${t.lowDose.naiveUp}, paired rule says none on ${t.lowDose.pairedNone}`);
    40	  out.push("", "## Cost and stability", "", `- total evaluation cost ${c.validNonNullYieldAndCost.totalCellSteps} cell-steps; cost per non-null case ${c.validNonNullYieldAndCost.costPerUseful === null ? "n/a" : c.validNonNullYieldAndCost.costPerUseful.toFixed(0)}`, `- cross-seed effect stability (qualify families, sibling seed): ${c.crossSeedEffectStability.agree}/${c.crossSeedEffectStability.n} labels agree (${f3(c.crossSeedEffectStability.rate)}); heterogeneity across seeds of one family, not a predictor's accuracy limit`, "", ...c.notes.map((n) => `- ${n}`), "");
    41	  return out.join("\n");
    42	}
    43	
    44	const jl = (xs: readonly unknown[]): string => xs.map((x) => canonical(x) + "\n").join("");
    45	
    46	export interface Built {
    47	  files: Record<string, string>;
    48	  canonicalSha256: string;
    49	  comparison: Comparison;
    50	  leaks: string[];
    51	}
    52	
    53	export function buildOutputs(p: Protocol, gen: Generated): Built {
    54	  if (!p.frozen && !p.devPrior) throw new Error("protocol is not frozen");
    55	  const envelopes: PredictionEnvelope[] = gen.results.flatMap((r) => r.envelopes);
    56	  const leaks = checkLeaks(p, envelopes);
    57	  if (leaks.length) throw new Error(`leak check failed: ${leaks.join("; ")}`);
    58	  const proposers = buildProposers(p, envelopes);
    59	  const eligible = devOnlyOracle(p);
    60	  const lines = runProposers(proposers, envelopes, eligible);
    61	  const comparison = compare(p, gen, envelopes, lines, proposers, eligible);
    62	  const rows = caseRows(p, gen.results);
    63	  const minEffect = p.minEffect!;
    64	  const pHash = protocolHash(p);
    65	  const files: Record<string, string> = {
    66	    "envelopes.jsonl": jl(envelopes),
    67	    "protocol.snapshot.json": canonical(p) + "\n",
    68	    "recipes.jsonl": jl([...gen.results, ...gen.variants].map((r) => {
    69	      const fam = p.families.find((f) => f.id === r.familyId)!;
    70	      const rec = recipeOf(p, fam, r.seed);
    71	      return { familyId: r.familyId, historyId: r.historyId, seed: r.seed, recipeHash: recipeHash(rec), controlHash: r.controlHash, neutralityChecks: r.neutralityChecks, recipe: rec };
    72	    })),
    73	    "predictions.jsonl": jl(lines),
    74	    "outcomes.jsonl": jl(rows.map((r) => ({ ...r, label: labelOf(r.effect.bodyMass, minEffect), measurementLimited: measurementLimited(r.effect.bodyMass, minEffect), protocolHash: pHash }))),
    75	    "comparison.json": canonical(comparison) + "\n",
    76	    "comparison.md": comparisonMarkdown(comparison, minEffect),
    77	  };
    78	  files["report.html"] = renderReport({ comparison, envelopes, lines, rows, minEffect, proposerIds: proposers.map((x) => x.id), eligible: (id, env) => eligible(proposers.find((x) => x.id === id)!, env) });
    79	  const canonicalSha256 = CANONICAL_FILES.map((n) => `${sha256Text(files[n])}  ${n}\n`).join("");
    80	  files["canonical.sha256"] = canonicalSha256;
    81	  return { files, canonicalSha256, comparison, leaks };
    82	}
    83	
    84	/**
    85	 * Re-renders the derived files (comparison.md, report.html, canonical.sha256) from the canonical data files already on disk:
    86	 * `read(name)` returns a file's text. The data files (envelopes, predictions, outcomes, comparison.json, protocol.snapshot.json)
    87	 * are never changed; the three derived files are a pure function of them and the renderer.
    88	 */
    89	export function rerender(read: (name: string) => string): Record<string, string> {
    90	  const parse = <T>(name: string): T[] => read(name).split("\n").filter((l) => l.trim() !== "").map((l) => JSON.parse(l) as T);
    91	  const comparison = JSON.parse(read("comparison.json")) as Comparison;
    92	  const protocol = JSON.parse(read("protocol.snapshot.json")) as Protocol;
    93	  const minEffect = protocol.minEffect!;
    94	  const envelopes = parse<PredictionEnvelope>("envelopes.jsonl");
    95	  const lines = parse<PredictionLine>("predictions.jsonl");
    96	  const rows = parse<CaseRow>("outcomes.jsonl");
    97	  const proposerIds = [...new Set(lines.map((l) => l.proposer))];
    98	  const oracleIds = new Set<string>(proposerIds.filter((id) => id === "mechanismRule"));
    99	  const plan = splitPlanOf(protocol);
   100	  const files: Record<string, string> = {};
   101	  for (const n of CANONICAL_FILES) if (n !== "comparison.md" && n !== "report.html") files[n] = read(n);
   102	  files["comparison.md"] = comparisonMarkdown(comparison, minEffect);
   103	  files["report.html"] = renderReport({ comparison, envelopes, lines, rows, minEffect, proposerIds, eligible: (id, env) => !oracleIds.has(id) || plan.dev.includes(env.familyId) });
   104	  const out: Record<string, string> = { "comparison.md": files["comparison.md"], "report.html": files["report.html"] };
   105	  out["canonical.sha256"] = CANONICAL_FILES.map((n) => `${sha256Text(files[n])}  ${n}\n`).join("");
   106	  return out;
   107	}
   108	export { BASELINE_IDS };
     1	// Static notebook view (PLAN.md, section 10): per case an observation block (census sparkline, frame thumbnails), a hypothesis
     2	// block (probabilities per proposer, with the free-text reason, escaped) and a measured-outcome block (the paired result), visibly
     3	// distinct. Failed predictions first; invalid or missing ones listed in their own section. No scripts, no external URLs, works from
     4	// file://. A pure function of its inputs.
     5	import { escapeHtml } from "../report-html/page.ts";
     6	import { labelOf } from "./fixtures.ts";
     7	import type { PredictionEnvelope } from "./envelope.ts";
     8	import type { CaseRow, Comparison, PredictionLine, SplitTable } from "./run.ts";
     9	import { argmax, validatePredictionFile } from "./validate.ts";
    10	
    11	const esc = escapeHtml;
    12	const pct = (v: number | null): string => (v === null ? "n/a" : v.toFixed(3));
    13	
    14	function spark(env: PredictionEnvelope): string {
    15	  const pts = env.census.map((c) => c.mass);
    16	  const lo = Math.min(...pts), hi = Math.max(...pts), span = Math.max(1, hi - lo);
    17	  const w = 160, h = 40;
    18	  const d = pts.map((v, i) => `${((i / Math.max(1, pts.length - 1)) * w).toFixed(1)},${(h - 2 - ((v - lo) / span) * (h - 4)).toFixed(1)}`).join(" ");
    19	  return `<svg class="spark" viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" role="img" aria-label="body mass over the observed steps ${env.census[0].step} to ${env.census[env.census.length - 1].step}"><polyline points="${d}" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5"/></svg>`;
    20	}
    21	/** The last frame as four-level quantised paths (one path per level, empty blocks omitted), to keep the page small. */
    22	function thumb(f: PredictionEnvelope["frames"][number]): string {
    23	  const max = Math.max(1, ...f.mass), s = 6;
    24	  const paths: string[] = ["", "", "", ""];
    25	  f.mass.forEach((m, i) => {
    26	    if (m <= 0) return;
    27	    const lvl = Math.min(3, Math.floor((m / max) * 4));
    28	    paths[lvl] += `M${(i % f.side) * s} ${Math.floor(i / f.side) * s}h${s}v${s}h-${s}z`;
    29	  });
    30	  const body = paths.map((d, i) => (d ? `<path d="${d}" fill="currentColor" fill-opacity="${[0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1][i]}"/>` : "")).join("");
    31	  return `<figure class="thumb"><svg viewBox="0 0 ${f.side * s} ${f.side * s}" role="img" aria-label="mass by block at step ${f.step}">${body}</svg><figcaption>t=${f.step}</figcaption></figure>`;
    32	}
    33	function bar(p: { up: number; down: number; none: number }): string {
    34	  return `<span class="bar" aria-hidden="true"><i class="up" style="width:${p.up / 10}%"></i><i class="down" style="width:${p.down / 10}%"></i><i class="none" style="width:${p.none / 10}%"></i></span>`;
    35	}
    36	
    37	function tableHtml(t: SplitTable): string {
    38	  const head = ["proposer", "role", "n", "answered", "abstained", "invalid", "missing", "coverage", "Brier (imputed)", "Brier (answered)", "direction acc.", "balanced acc.", "acc. @ matched coverage", "valid non-null yield"];
    39	  const body = t.rows.map((r) => `<tr><th scope="row">${esc(r.id)}</th><td>${esc(r.role)}</td><td>${r.n}</td><td>${r.answered}</td><td>${r.abstained}</td><td>${r.invalid}</td><td>${r.missing}</td><td>${pct(r.coverage)}</td><td>${pct(r.brierImputed)}</td><td>${pct(r.brierAnswered)}</td><td>${pct(r.directionalAccuracy)}</td><td>${pct(r.balancedAccuracy)}</td><td>${pct(r.accuracyAtMatchedCoverage)}</td><td>${pct(r.validNonNullYield)}</td></tr>`).join("");
    40	  return `<h3>${esc(t.split)} (${t.nCases} cases, ${t.nFamilies} families; up ${t.classCounts.up}, down ${t.classCounts.down}, none ${t.classCounts.none})</h3><div class="scroll"><table><thead><tr>${head.map((h) => `<th scope="col">${esc(h)}</th>`).join("")}</tr></thead><tbody>${body}</tbody></table></div>`;
    41	}
    42	
    43	export interface ReportInput {
    44	  comparison: Comparison;
    45	  envelopes: readonly PredictionEnvelope[];
    46	  lines: readonly PredictionLine[];
    47	  rows: readonly CaseRow[];
    48	  minEffect: number;
    49	  proposerIds: readonly string[];
    50	  /** Whether a proposer was run on an envelope (the dev-only oracle is not shown, and not counted missing, on test cases). Default: always. */
    51	  eligible?: (proposerId: string, env: PredictionEnvelope) => boolean;
    52	}
    53	
    54	export function renderReport(inp: ReportInput): string {
    55	  const { comparison: c, envelopes, lines, rows, minEffect } = inp;
    56	  const eligible = inp.eligible ?? (() => true);
    57	  const proposerIds = inp.proposerIds;
    58	  const idsFor = (e: PredictionEnvelope): string[] => proposerIds.filter((id) => eligible(id, e));
    59	  const rowOf = new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.caseId, r]));
    60	  // Per-proposer validation (so invalid lines show up here as they do in the scorer).
    61	  const status = new Map<string, Map<string, "answered" | "abstained" | "invalid" | "missing">>();
    62	  const preds = new Map<string, Map<string, PredictionLine["prediction"] | null>>();
    63	  for (const id of proposerIds) {
    64	    const chk = validatePredictionFile(lines.filter((l) => l.proposer === id).map((l) => JSON.stringify(l.prediction)), envelopes);
    65	    const s = new Map<string, "answered" | "abstained" | "invalid" | "missing">(), pm = new Map<string, PredictionLine["prediction"] | null>();
    66	    for (const e of envelopes) {
    67	      const g = chk.byCase.get(e.caseId);
    68	      s.set(e.caseId, !g ? "missing" : !g.prediction ? "invalid" : g.prediction.abstain ? "abstained" : "answered");
    69	      pm.set(e.caseId, g?.prediction ?? null);
    70	    }
    71	    status.set(id, s);
    72	    preds.set(id, pm);
    73	  }
    74	  const envOf = new Map(envelopes.map((e) => [e.caseId, e]));
    75	  const failures = (caseId: string): number => {
    76	    const r = rowOf.get(caseId)!;
    77	    const o = labelOf(r.effect.bodyMass, minEffect);
    78	    let k = 0;
    79	    for (const id of idsFor(envOf.get(caseId)!)) {
    80	      const st = status.get(id)!.get(caseId)!;
    81	      const p = preds.get(id)!.get(caseId);
    82	      if (st === "invalid" || st === "missing" || (st === "answered" && p?.probs && argmax(p.probs) !== o)) k++;
    83	    }
    84	    return k;
    85	  };
    86	  const ordered = [...envelopes].filter((e) => rowOf.has(e.caseId)).sort((a, b) => failures(b.caseId) - failures(a.caseId) || (a.caseId < b.caseId ? -1 : 1));
    87	  const caseHtml = (e: PredictionEnvelope): string => {
    88	    const r = rowOf.get(e.caseId)!;
    89	    const o = labelOf(r.effect.bodyMass, minEffect);
    90	    const hyp = idsFor(e)
    91	      .map((id) => {
    92	        const st = status.get(id)!.get(e.caseId)!;
    93	        const p = preds.get(id)!.get(e.caseId);
    94	        if (!p || st === "invalid" || st === "missing") return `<li class="bad"><b>${esc(id)}</b> ${esc(st)}</li>`;
    95	        if (p.abstain) return `<li><b>${esc(id)}</b> abstains <q>${esc(p.reason ?? "")}</q></li>`;
    96	        const a = argmax(p.probs!);
    97	        return `<li class="${a === o ? "ok" : "bad"}"><b>${esc(id)}</b> ${bar(p.probs!)} up ${p.probs!.up} / down ${p.probs!.down} / none ${p.probs!.none} per-mille, says ${esc(a)} <q>${esc(p.reason ?? "")}</q></li>`;
    98	      })
    99	      .join("");
   100	    const op = e.task.op;
   101	    const opText = op.kind === "none" ? "no operation (sham)" : `${op.kind} at (${op.x}, ${op.y}) radius ${op.r}${op.amount !== undefined ? `, amount ${op.amount}` : ""}`;
   102	    return `<article id="${esc(e.caseId)}" class="case"><h3>${esc(e.caseId)} <small>family ${esc(e.familyId)}, ${esc(r.split)}</small></h3>
   103	<section class="obs"><h4>Observation (steps up to ${e.task.forkStep})</h4>${spark(e)}<div class="thumbs">${thumb(e.frames[e.frames.length - 1])}</div></section>
   104	<section class="hyp"><h4>Hypotheses</h4><p>Question: ${esc(opText)} at step ${e.task.forkStep + e.task.delta}; direction of the ${esc(e.task.readout)} difference at step ${e.task.forkStep + e.task.horizon}.</p><ul>${hyp}</ul></section>
   105	<section class="out"><h4>Measured outcome</h4><p>treated ${r.treated}, control ${r.control}, effect ${r.effect.bodyMass} (minimum effect ${minEffect}) so <b>${esc(o)}</b>; fixture expectation ${esc(r.expected)}${o === r.expected ? "" : " (measured differs)"}.</p></section></article>`;
   106	  };
   107	  const bad = ordered.filter((e) => idsFor(e).some((id) => ["invalid", "missing"].includes(status.get(id)!.get(e.caseId)!)));
   108	  const css = `body{font:15px/1.5 system-ui,sans-serif;max-width:72rem;margin:0 auto;padding:1rem;color:#1d2328;background:#fbfaf6}table{border-collapse:collapse;font-size:13px}th,td{border:1px solid #cfc9bb;padding:.25rem .5rem;text-align:left}.scroll{overflow-x:auto}.case{border:1px solid #cfc9bb;border-radius:6px;margin:1rem 0;padding:.5rem 1rem;display:grid;gap:.5rem}.obs{background:#eef3f6;padding:.5rem;border-radius:4px;color:#27475a}.hyp{background:#fbf3e3;padding:.5rem;border-radius:4px}.out{background:#e8f2e8;padding:.5rem;border-radius:4px}.spark{width:10rem;height:2.5rem}.thumbs{display:flex;gap:.5rem;flex-wrap:wrap}.thumb{margin:0}.thumb svg{width:3.5rem;height:3.5rem;background:#fff;border:1px solid #cfc9bb}.thumb figcaption{font-size:11px}ul{padding-left:1rem;margin:.25rem 0}li.ok b{color:#1f6b2a}li.bad b{color:#9b2c1c}q{color:#555}.bar{display:inline-flex;width:6rem;height:.6rem;vertical-align:middle;margin-right:.4rem;background:#ddd}.bar i{display:block;height:100%}.bar .up{background:#2a7a3a}.bar .down{background:#b23b2a}.bar .none{background:#8a8f94}small{color:#666;font-weight:normal}code{overflow-wrap:anywhere}`;
   109	  return `<!doctype html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><title>Prediction envelope v0: notebook</title><style>${css}</style></head><body>
   110	<h1>Prediction envelope v0: notebook</h1>
   111	<p>${esc(c.noModelStatement)}</p>
   112	<p>Protocol hash <code>${esc(c.protocolHash)}</code>. Strongest simple baseline on dev: <b>${esc(c.strongestBaselineOnDev)}</b>. Primary readout: body mass (B+P), minimum effect ${minEffect}. Failed predictions first.</p>
   113	<p>The mechanism oracle is a dev diagnostic: it is run on, and shown for, dev cases only.</p>
   114	<h2>Comparison</h2>${tableHtml(c.dev)}${tableHtml(c.test)}${tableHtml(c.testExcludingMeasurementLimited)}
   115	<h2>Invalid or missing predictions</h2>${bad.length ? `<ul>${bad.map((e) => `<li><a href="#${esc(e.caseId)}">${esc(e.caseId)}</a></li>`).join("")}</ul>` : "<p>None.</p>"}
   116	<h2>Cases</h2>${ordered.map(caseHtml).join("\n")}
   117	</body></html>
   118	`;
   119	}

     1	// The host validator (PLAN.md, section 3). A prediction is accepted only if it is exactly the schema, restates the envelope's one
     2	// frozen task verbatim, and its task converts to operations the existing runner validator accepts. Invalid proposals are counted
     3	// and scored as non-answers; nothing is ever repaired. The sham ({kind:"none"}) is an explicit branch that maps to NO scheduled
     4	// operation (the existing replay control omits the schedule), never to a radius-0 lesion or a zero feed.
     5	import { cellCount, worldW } from "@bl/schema";
     6	import { interventionsError, validateInterventions, type ScheduledIntervention } from "@bl/runner";
     7	import { canonical } from "../propose.ts";
     8	import { CLASSES, ENVELOPE_VERSION, PREDICTION_VERSION, envelopeIntact, type Direction3, type PredictionEnvelope, type Task } from "./envelope.ts";
     9	
    10	export interface Probs {
    11	  up: number;
    12	  down: number;
    13	  none: number;
    14	}
    15	export interface Prediction {
    16	  version: number;
    17	  caseId: string;
    18	  envelopeHash: string;
    19	  task: Task;
    20	  probs?: Probs;
    21	  abstain: boolean;
    22	  reason?: string;
    23	}
    24	export interface Validation {
    25	  ok: boolean;
    26	  errors: string[];
    27	}
    28	
    29	const PRED_KEYS = ["version", "caseId", "envelopeHash", "task", "probs", "abstain", "reason"];
    30	const FORBIDDEN = ["__proto__", "constructor", "prototype"];
    31	const isObj = (v: unknown): v is Record<string, unknown> => typeof v === "object" && v !== null && !Array.isArray(v);
    32	/** A JSON-compatible record: a plain object (Object.prototype or null prototype), so every field it has is its own and survives JSON.stringify. */
    33	const isPlain = (v: unknown): v is Record<string, unknown> => {
    34	  if (!isObj(v)) return false;
    35	  const proto = Object.getPrototypeOf(v);
    36	  return proto === Object.prototype || proto === null;
    37	};
    38	
    39	/** The scheduled operations a task asks for: null for the sham, else the list `validateInterventions` takes. Errors name the task, never repair it. */
    40	export function taskSchedule(env: PredictionEnvelope): { schedule: ScheduledIntervention[] | null; errors: string[] } {
    41	  const t = env.task, op = t.op, cfg = env.config, errors: string[] = [];
    42	  const W = worldW(cfg), H = cellCount(cfg) / W;
    43	  const entry = env.menu.ops.find((m) => m.kind === op.kind);
    44	  if (!entry) return { schedule: null, errors: [`operation ${JSON.stringify(op.kind)} is not on the menu`] };
    45	  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(t.delta) || t.delta < env.menu.deltaMin || t.delta > env.menu.deltaMax) errors.push(`delta ${t.delta} is outside the menu's ${env.menu.deltaMin}..${env.menu.deltaMax}`);
    46	  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(t.horizon) || t.horizon < t.delta) errors.push(`horizon ${t.horizon} must be at least delta ${t.delta}`);
    47	  if (op.kind === "none") {
    48	    for (const k of Object.keys(op)) if (k !== "kind") errors.push(`a sham has no key ${JSON.stringify(k)}`);
    49	    return { schedule: null, errors };
    50	  }
    51	  const want = op.kind === "lesion" ? ["kind", "x", "y", "r"] : ["kind", "x", "y", "r", "amount"];
    52	  for (const k of Object.keys(op)) if (!want.includes(k)) errors.push(`a ${op.kind} has no key ${JSON.stringify(k)}`);
    53	  for (const k of want) if (!Object.hasOwn(op, k)) errors.push(`a ${op.kind} needs the key ${JSON.stringify(k)}`);
    54	  if (errors.length) return { schedule: null, errors };
    55	  const { x, y, r } = op as { x: number; y: number; r: number };
    56	  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(r) || r < (entry.rMin ?? 1) || r > (entry.rMax ?? 0)) errors.push(`radius ${r} is outside the menu's ${entry.rMin}..${entry.rMax}`);
    57	  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(x) || x < 0 || x >= W) errors.push(`x ${x} is outside the world`);
    58	  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(y) || y < 0 || y >= H) errors.push(`y ${y} is outside the world`);
    59	  let entryOut: ScheduledIntervention;
    60	  const step = t.forkStep + t.delta;
    61	  if (op.kind === "lesion") entryOut = { step, kind: "lesion", x, y, r };
    62	  else {
    63	    const a = op.amount as number;
    64	    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(a) || a < (entry.amountMin ?? 1) || a > (entry.amountMax ?? 0)) errors.push(`amount ${a} is outside the menu's ${entry.amountMin}..${entry.amountMax}`);
    65	    entryOut = { step, kind: "feed", x, y, r, amount: op.kind === "drain" ? -a : a };
    66	  }
    67	  if (errors.length) return { schedule: null, errors };
    68	  const schedule = [entryOut];
    69	  const bad = validateInterventions(schedule);
    70	  if (bad.length) return { schedule: null, errors: bad };
    71	  const world = interventionsError({ interventions: schedule }, cfg, t.forkStep);
    72	  if (world) return { schedule: null, errors: [world] };
    73	  return { schedule, errors: [] };
    74	}
    75	
    76	/** An envelope must itself be sound: right version, intact hash, a task its own menu allows. Builders assert this; the validator trusts only a sound one. */
    77	export function validateEnvelope(env: PredictionEnvelope): Validation {
    78	  const errors: string[] = [];
    79	  if (env.version !== ENVELOPE_VERSION) errors.push(`envelope version ${env.version}`);
    80	  if (!envelopeIntact(env)) errors.push("envelope hash does not match its content");
    81	  errors.push(...taskSchedule(env).errors);
    82	  return { ok: errors.length === 0, errors };
    83	}
    84	
    85	export function validatePrediction(p: unknown, env: PredictionEnvelope): Validation {
    86	  const errors: string[] = [];
    87	  if (!isPlain(p)) return { ok: false, errors: ["a prediction must be a plain JSON object (no inherited fields)"] };
    88	  for (const k of Object.keys(p)) {
    89	    if (FORBIDDEN.includes(k)) errors.push(`forbidden key ${JSON.stringify(k)}`);
    90	    else if (!PRED_KEYS.includes(k)) errors.push(`unknown key ${JSON.stringify(k)}`);
    91	  }
    92	  if (errors.length) return { ok: false, errors };
    93	  // Required fields must be the object's own: an inherited field would validate but serialize as absent.
    94	  for (const k of ["version", "caseId", "envelopeHash", "task", "abstain"]) if (!Object.hasOwn(p, k)) errors.push(`missing own field ${JSON.stringify(k)}`);
    95	  if (errors.length) return { ok: false, errors };
    96	  let stable = false;
    97	  try {
    98	    stable = canonical(p) === canonical(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(p)));
    99	  } catch {
   100	    stable = false;
   101	  }
   102	  if (!stable) return { ok: false, errors: ["the prediction does not survive JSON serialization unchanged"] };
   103	  if (p.version !== PREDICTION_VERSION) errors.push(`version must be ${PREDICTION_VERSION}`);
   104	  if (p.caseId !== env.caseId) errors.push(`caseId ${JSON.stringify(p.caseId)} is not ${JSON.stringify(env.caseId)}`);
   105	  if (p.envelopeHash !== env.envelopeHash) errors.push("envelopeHash does not match the envelope");
   106	  if (!isPlain(p.task)) errors.push("task is missing or not a plain object");
   107	  else {
   108	    let same = false;
   109	    try {
   110	      same = canonical(p.task) === canonical(env.task);
   111	    } catch {
   112	      same = false;
   113	    }
   114	    if (!same) errors.push("task does not restate the envelope's task verbatim (the question is fixed by the host)");
   115	  }
   116	  if (typeof p.abstain !== "boolean") errors.push("abstain must be a boolean");
   117	  if (p.reason !== undefined && typeof p.reason !== "string") errors.push("reason must be a string");
   118	  if (p.abstain === true) {
   119	    if (Object.hasOwn(p, "probs")) errors.push("an abstention carries no probs");
   120	  } else if (p.abstain === false) {
   121	    if (!isPlain(p.probs)) errors.push("probs are required unless abstaining, as a plain object");
   122	    else {
   123	      const pr = p.probs;
   124	      for (const k of Object.keys(pr)) if (!(CLASSES as readonly string[]).includes(k)) errors.push(`probs has unknown key ${JSON.stringify(k)}`);
   125	      let sum = 0;
   126	      for (const c of CLASSES) {
   127	        const v = Object.hasOwn(pr, c) ? pr[c] : undefined;
   128	        if (!Number.isInteger(v) || (v as number) < 0 || (v as number) > 1000) errors.push(`probs.${c} must be an integer in 0..1000 (per-mille)`);
   129	        else sum += v as number;
   130	      }
   131	      if (errors.length === 0 && sum !== 1000) errors.push(`probs must sum to exactly 1000, got ${sum}`);
   132	    }
   133	  }
   134	  return { ok: errors.length === 0, errors };
   135	}
   136	
   137	/** argmax of the stated probs, tie order none > down > up (derived, never submitted). */
   138	export function argmax(p: Probs): Direction3 {
   139	  let best: Direction3 = "none";
   140	  for (const c of ["none", "down", "up"] as const) if (p[c] > p[best]) best = c;
   141	  return best;
   142	}
   143	
   144	export interface FileCheck {
   145	  /** caseId -> prediction-or-null (null: invalid, or no valid line). */
   146	  byCase: Map<string, { prediction: Prediction | null; errors: string[] }>;
   147	  invalid: number;
   148	  missing: string[];
   149	  extra: string[];
   150	  duplicates: string[];
   151	  notObject: number;
   152	  /** Envelopes that fail validateEnvelope (version, recomputed hash, task on its own menu): their cases cannot be answered validly. */
   153	  badEnvelopes: string[];
   154	}
   155	
   156	/** File-level validation: exactly one line per case. Extras, duplicates and missing cases are counted; a duplicated case scores as invalid. */
   157	export function validatePredictionFile(lines: string[], envelopes: readonly PredictionEnvelope[]): FileCheck {
   158	  const byEnv = new Map(envelopes.map((e) => [e.caseId, e]));
   159	  const out: FileCheck = { byCase: new Map(), invalid: 0, missing: [], extra: [], duplicates: [], notObject: 0, badEnvelopes: [] };
   160	  const unsound = new Map<string, string[]>();
   161	  for (const e of envelopes) {
   162	    const v = validateEnvelope(e);
   163	    if (!v.ok) {
   164	      unsound.set(e.caseId, v.errors);
   165	      out.badEnvelopes.push(e.caseId);
   166	    }
   167	  }
   168	  const seen = new Set<string>();
   169	  for (const line of lines) {
   170	    if (line.trim() === "") continue;
   171	    let raw: unknown;
   172	    try {
   173	      raw = JSON.parse(line);
   174	    } catch {
   175	      out.notObject++;
   176	      out.invalid++;
   177	      continue;
   178	    }
   179	    if (!isPlain(raw)) {
   180	      out.notObject++;
   181	      out.invalid++;
   182	      continue;
   183	    }
   184	    const id = typeof raw.caseId === "string" ? raw.caseId : "";
   185	    const env = byEnv.get(id);
   186	    if (!env) {
   187	      out.extra.push(id);
   188	      out.invalid++;
   189	      continue;
   190	    }
   191	    if (seen.has(id)) {
   192	      out.duplicates.push(id);
   193	      out.byCase.set(id, { prediction: null, errors: ["duplicate line for this case"] });
   194	      out.invalid++;
   195	      continue;
   196	    }
   197	    seen.add(id);
   198	    const v = unsound.has(id) ? { ok: false, errors: ["the envelope is unsound: " + unsound.get(id)!.join("; ")] } : validatePrediction(raw, env);
   199	    if (!v.ok) out.invalid++;
   200	    out.byCase.set(id, { prediction: v.ok ? (raw as unknown as Prediction) : null, errors: v.errors });
   201	  }
   202	  for (const e of envelopes) if (!seen.has(e.caseId)) out.missing.push(e.caseId);
   203	  return out;
   204	}

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	// Known-mechanism fixtures with outcomes from paired CPU replays (PLAN.md, section 6). Small worlds (32 x 32, kernelRadius 4) run
     2	// on `RefSim`. For one source history (a family) the generator
     3	//   1. runs the history twice to the fork step C: instrumented (census series and frame samples taken every few steps) and plain
     4	//      (state hashes only), and requires the two to agree on the full-state hash at every sampled step (observer neutrality);
     5	//   2. builds one envelope per mechanism case from the instrumented run (only data at steps <= C goes in);
     6	//   3. from the plain state at C runs a control arm (no operation) and, per case, a treated arm (advance to C+delta, apply, advance
     7	//      to C+H), every arm on its own deep clone, and records effect = readout(treated) - readout(control) at C+H.
     8	// Proposers see envelopes; only the scorer reads `effect`. Labels are derived from `effect` and the frozen `minEffect` at scoring time.
     9	import { CH, RULE_VERSION, PRESETS, buildWorld, cellCount, generalistGenome, isLiving, lowbias32, presetConfig, stateHash, totalsOf, worldW, cloneState, type Founder, type WorldState } from "@bl/schema";
    10	import { RefSim } from "@bl/sim-ref";

    12	import type { ScheduledIntervention } from "@bl/runner";

    14	import { censusPoint, frameOf, sealEnvelope, standardMenu, type Direction3, type OpKind, type PredictionEnvelope, type Task, type TaskOp } from "./envelope.ts";
    15	import { freeNutrient, livingCells, totalMass, type DiagnosticId, type ReadoutId } from "./readouts.ts";

    49	  world: WorldSpec;
    50	  window: WindowSpec;

    53	  seedVariantOffset: number;

    57	export interface FixtureHooks {
    58	  observer?: (state: WorldState, step: number, phase: "history" | "continuation") => void;

    61	/** Bounds a protocol must satisfy before anything is simulated (PLAN.md, section 6: caps and bounds are checked first, variants included). */
    62	export function assertFixtureBounds(p: FixtureProtocol): void {
    63	  const int = (v: unknown, lo: number, hi: number, what: string): void => {
    64	    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(v) || (v as number) < lo || (v as number) > hi) throw new Error(`protocol ${what} ${String(v)} is outside ${lo}..${hi}`);
    65	  };
    66	  const { world, window: w, menu } = p;
    67	  int(world.side, 8, 256, "world.side");
    68	  int(world.kernelRadius, 1, 16, "world.kernelRadius");
    69	  int(w.forkStep, 1, 100_000, "window.forkStep");
    70	  int(w.delta, 1, 10_000, "window.delta");
    71	  int(w.horizon, w.delta, 100_000, "window.horizon");
    72	  int(w.censusEvery, 1, w.forkStep, "window.censusEvery");
    73	  for (const f of w.frameSteps) int(f, 0, w.forkStep, "window.frameSteps entry");
    74	  int(menu.rMax, 1, 64, "menu.rMax");
    75	  int(menu.amountMax, 1, 1 << 24, "menu.amountMax");
    76	  if (p.mechanisms.length < 1 || p.mechanisms.length > 64) throw new Error("protocol needs 1..64 mechanisms");
    77	  for (const m of p.mechanisms) {
    78	    if (m.kind !== "none") int(m.r, 1, menu.rMax, `mechanism ${m.id} r`);
    79	    if (m.kind === "feed" || m.kind === "drain") int(m.amount, 1, menu.amountMax, `mechanism ${m.id} amount`);
    80	  }
    81	}
    82	
    83	/** Cell-steps one family run will simulate, every arm and check included: known before anything runs. */
    84	export function plannedCellSteps(p: FixtureProtocol): number {
    85	  assertFixtureBounds(p);
    86	  const n = p.world.side * p.world.side;
    87	  const { forkStep: C, horizon: H } = p.window;
    88	  // two history runs to C; control + one arm per mechanism, each with an instrumented twin.
    89	  return n * (2 * C + 2 * (1 + p.mechanisms.length) * H);
    90	}
    91	
    92	export function makeWorld(p: FixtureProtocol, fam: FamilySpec, seed = fam.seed): WorldState {
    93	  const preset = PRESETS.find((x) => x.id === fam.light)!;
    94	  const cfg = presetConfig(preset, seed, { tileW: p.world.side, tileH: p.world.side, kernelRadius: p.world.kernelRadius });
    95	  const W = p.world.side;
    96	  const founders: Founder[] = [];
    97	  for (let i = 0; i < fam.founders; i++) {
    98	    const h = lowbias32(seed * 31 + i);
    99	    founders.push({ x: h % W, y: (h >>> 12) % W, radius: p.world.founderRadius, genome: generalistGenome(cfg.defaultMu, cfg.defaultSigma), biomass: p.world.founderBiomass, energy: p.world.founderEnergy });
   100	  }
   101	  return buildWorld(cfg, { nutrient: fam.nutrient, founders });
   102	}
   103	
   104	/** Cell index of the heaviest cell (B+P), ties to the lowest index. */
   105	export function heaviestCell(s: WorldState): { x: number; y: number } {
   106	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg), W = worldW(s.cfg);
   107	  let best = -1, bi = 0;
   108	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   109	    const m = s.cells[CH.B * n + i] + s.cells[CH.P * n + i];
   110	    if (m > best) {
   111	      best = m;
   112	      bi = i;
   113	    }
   114	  }
   115	  return { x: bi % W, y: Math.floor(bi / W) };
   116	}
   117	
   118	/** The cell farthest (wrapped squared distance) from every living cell; ties to the lowest index. If nothing lives, the world centre. */
   119	export function emptiestCell(s: WorldState): { x: number; y: number } {
   120	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg), W = worldW(s.cfg), H = n / W;
   121	  const live: number[] = [];
   122	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) if (isLiving(s, i)) live.push(i);
   123	  if (live.length === 0) return { x: W >> 1, y: H >> 1 };
   124	  let best = -1, bi = 0;
   125	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   126	    const x = i % W, y = Math.floor(i / W);
   127	    let d = Infinity;
   128	    for (const j of live) {
   129	      const dx = Math.min(Math.abs(x - (j % W)), W - Math.abs(x - (j % W)));
   130	      const dy = Math.min(Math.abs(y - Math.floor(j / W)), H - Math.abs(y - Math.floor(j / W)));
   131	      d = Math.min(d, dx * dx + dy * dy);
   132	    }
   133	    if (d > best) {
   134	      best = d;
   135	      bi = i;
   136	    }
   137	  }
   138	  return { x: bi % W, y: Math.floor(bi / W) };
   139	}
   140	
   141	export function taskOpFor(m: MechanismSpec, at: { x: number; y: number } | null): TaskOp {
   142	  if (m.kind === "none") return { kind: "none" };
   143	  const x = at!.x, y = at!.y, r = m.r!;
   144	  return m.kind === "lesion" ? { kind: "lesion", x, y, r } : { kind: m.kind, x, y, r, amount: m.amount! };
   145	}
   146	
   147	export interface ArmRecord {
   148	  caseId: string;
   149	  mechanismId: string;
   150	  effect: Record<ReadoutId | DiagnosticId, number>;
   151	  treated: Record<ReadoutId | DiagnosticId, number>;
   152	  control: Record<ReadoutId | DiagnosticId, number>;
   153	  /** Hash of the treated arm's end state; for the sham equals the control's. */
   154	  treatedHash: string;
   155	  applied: { kind: string; deliveredMatter: number; refused: string | null; radius: number }[];
   156	  energyResidual: string;
   157	  matterResidual: string;
   158	}
   159	export interface FamilyResult {
   160	  familyId: string;
   161	  historyId: string;
   162	  seed: number;
   163	  envelopes: PredictionEnvelope[];
   164	  arms: ArmRecord[];
   165	  controlHash: string;
   166	  /** Hashes compared between instrumented and plain history runs, and how many sampled steps they covered. */
   167	  neutralityChecks: number;
   168	  /** Simulated cell-steps spent on this family, every arm and check included. */
   169	  cellSteps: number;
   170	}
   171	
   172	export const caseIdOf = (familyId: string, mechanismId: string): string => "c" + sha256Text(`${familyId}|${mechanismId}`).slice(0, 12);
   173	
   174	/** Runs one family. `variantSeed` replaces the family's seed (for the cross-seed stability check); the family id stays. */
   175	export function runFamily(p: FixtureProtocol, fam: FamilySpec, variantSeed?: number, hooks?: FixtureHooks): FamilyResult {
   176	  const planned = plannedCellSteps(p);
   177	  const seed = variantSeed ?? fam.seed;
   178	  const { forkStep: C, delta, horizon: H, censusEvery, frameSteps } = p.window;
   179	  const n = cellCount(makeWorld(p, fam, seed).cfg);
   180	  let cellSteps = 0;
   181	  const world0 = makeWorld(p, fam, seed);
   182	  const startTotals = totalsOf(world0.cfg, world0.cells);
   183	
   184	  // The sampled steps: every census step, plus the frame steps (all <= C).
   185	  const sampled = [...new Set([...Array.from({ length: Math.floor(C / censusEvery) }, (_, i) => (i + 1) * censusEvery), ...frameSteps, C])].sort((a, b) => a - b);
   186	
   187	  // Instrumented history.
   188	  const inst = new RefSim(cloneState(world0));
   189	  const census = [censusPoint(inst.state, livingCells(inst.state), freeNutrient(inst.state), totalMass(inst.state))];
   190	  const frames = frameSteps.includes(0) ? [frameOf(inst.state)] : [];
   191	  const instHash: string[] = [];
   192	  for (const t of sampled) {
   193	    inst.run(t - inst.state.step);
   194	    hooks?.observer?.(inst.state, t, "history");
   195	    if (t % censusEvery === 0) census.push(censusPoint(inst.state, livingCells(inst.state), freeNutrient(inst.state), totalMass(inst.state)));
   196	    if (frameSteps.includes(t)) frames.push(frameOf(inst.state));
   197	    instHash.push(stateHash(inst.state));
   198	  }
   199	  cellSteps += C * n;
   200	  // Plain history: hashes only.
   201	  const plain = new RefSim(cloneState(world0));
   202	  const plainHash: string[] = [];
   203	  for (const t of sampled) {
   204	    plain.run(t - plain.state.step);
   205	    plainHash.push(stateHash(plain.state));
   206	  }
   207	  cellSteps += C * n;
   208	  for (let i = 0; i < sampled.length; i++) if (instHash[i] !== plainHash[i]) throw new Error(`observer neutrality failed for ${fam.id} at step ${sampled[i]}`);
   209	  const forkState = plain.state;
   210	
   211	  const historyId = `${fam.id}:s${seed}`;
   212	  // Control arm.
   213	  const ctl = new CpuInterventionAdapter(forkState, startTotals);
   214	  ctl.advanceTo(C + H);
   215	  cellSteps += H * n;
   216	  checkBooks(ctl, `${fam.id} control`);
   217	  const control = { bodyMass: totalMass(ctl.state), livingCells: livingCells(ctl.state), freeNutrient: freeNutrient(ctl.state) };
   218	  const controlHash = stateHash(ctl.state);
   219	  cellSteps += twinCheck(forkState, startTotals, null, C, C + H, censusEvery, controlHash, `${fam.id} control`, hooks) * n;
   220	
   221	  const occupied = heaviestCell(forkState), empty = emptiestCell(forkState);
   222	  const envelopes: PredictionEnvelope[] = [];
   223	  const arms: ArmRecord[] = [];
   224	  for (const m of p.mechanisms) {
   225	    const at = m.where === "occupied" ? occupied : m.where === "empty" ? empty : null;
   226	    const op = taskOpFor(m, at);
   227	    const task: Task = { subject: "world", op, delta, readout: "bodyMass", forkStep: C, horizon: H };
   228	    const env = sealEnvelope({
   229	      version: 1,
   230	      caseId: caseIdOf(fam.id, m.id),
   231	      historyId,
   232	      familyId: fam.id,
   233	      ruleVersion: RULE_VERSION,
   234	      config: inst.cfg,
   235	      census,
   236	      frames,
   237	      menu: standardMenu(inst.cfg, delta + 50, p.menu.amountMax, p.menu.rMax),
   238	      task,
   239	    });
   240	    const ts = taskSchedule(env);
   241	    if (ts.errors.length) throw new Error(`fixture ${env.caseId} (${m.id}) is not a valid task: ${ts.errors.join("; ")}`);
   242	    envelopes.push(env);
   243	
   244	    const arm = new CpuInterventionAdapter(forkState, startTotals);
   245	    if (ts.schedule) {
   246	      arm.advanceTo(ts.schedule[0].step);
   247	      arm.apply(ts.schedule[0]);
   248	    }
   249	    arm.advanceTo(C + H);
   250	    cellSteps += H * n;
   251	    checkBooks(arm, `${fam.id} ${m.id}`);
   252	    const treated = { bodyMass: totalMass(arm.state), livingCells: livingCells(arm.state), freeNutrient: freeNutrient(arm.state) };
   253	    const treatedHash = stateHash(arm.state);
   254	    cellSteps += twinCheck(forkState, startTotals, ts.schedule ? ts.schedule[0] : null, C, C + H, censusEvery, treatedHash, `${fam.id} ${m.id}`, hooks) * n;
   255	    if (m.kind === "none" && treatedHash !== controlHash) throw new Error(`sham twin differs from control for ${fam.id}`);
   256	    arms.push({
   257	      caseId: env.caseId,
   258	      mechanismId: m.id,
   259	      effect: { bodyMass: treated.bodyMass - control.bodyMass, livingCells: treated.livingCells - control.livingCells, freeNutrient: treated.freeNutrient - control.freeNutrient },
   260	      treated,
   261	      control,
   262	      treatedHash,
   263	      applied: arm.log.map((l) => ({ kind: l.kind, deliveredMatter: l.deliveredMatter, refused: l.refused, radius: l.radius })),
   264	      energyResidual: arm.energyResidual().toString(),
   265	      matterResidual: arm.matterResidual().toString(),
   266	    });
   267	  }
   268	  if (cellSteps !== planned) throw new Error(`${fam.id}: simulated ${cellSteps} cell-steps, planned ${planned}`);
   269	  return { familyId: fam.id, historyId, seed, envelopes, arms, controlHash, neutralityChecks: sampled.length + 1 + p.mechanisms.length, cellSteps };
   270	}
   271	
   272	/**
   273	 * Observer neutrality through the endpoint (C+H), not just to C: an independent continuation from the fork with the same schedule,
   274	 * advanced in observer-sized chunks with census/frame/readout instrumentation switched on, must end on the same full-state hash as
   275	 * the plain arm. Returns the steps simulated (to be multiplied by the cell count).
   276	 */
   277	export function twinCheck(fork: WorldState, startTotals: { matter: bigint; energy: bigint }, op: ScheduledIntervention | null, from: number, end: number, every: number, plainFinalHash: string, what: string, hooks?: FixtureHooks): number {
   278	  const tw = new CpuInterventionAdapter(fork, startTotals);
   279	  const stops = new Set<number>([end]);
   280	  for (let t = from + every; t < end; t += every) stops.add(t);
   281	  if (op) stops.add(op.step);
   282	  const observe = (): void => {
   283	    const s = tw.state;
   284	    censusPoint(s, livingCells(s), freeNutrient(s), totalMass(s));
   285	    frameOf(s);
   286	    hooks?.observer?.(s, s.step, "continuation");
   287	  };
   288	  for (const t of [...stops].sort((a, b) => a - b)) {
   289	    tw.advanceTo(t);
   290	    observe();
   291	    if (op && t === op.step) {
   292	      tw.apply(op);
   293	      observe();
   294	    }
   295	  }
   296	  const h = stateHash(tw.state);
   297	  if (h !== plainFinalHash) throw new Error(`observer neutrality failed for ${what}: instrumented continuation ends on ${h}, plain on ${plainFinalHash}`);
   298	  return end - from;
   299	}
   300	
   301	export function checkBooks(a: Pick<CpuInterventionAdapter, "energyResidual" | "matterResidual">, what: string): void {
   302	  if (a.energyResidual() !== 0n) throw new Error(`${what}: energy ledger residual ${a.energyResidual()}`);
   303	  if (a.matterResidual() !== 0n) throw new Error(`${what}: matter residual ${a.matterResidual()}`);
   304	}

   306	/** Label of an effect against a minimum effect in the readout's own unit. Inclusive at the boundary: an effect of exactly +minEffect is "up", exactly -minEffect is "down" (stated in PLAN.md, amendment 1). */
   307	export function labelOf(effect: number, minEffect: number): Direction3 {
   308	  return effect >= minEffect ? "up" : effect <= -minEffect ? "down" : "none";
   309	}

    35	export const protocolHash = (p: Protocol): string => sha256Text(canonical(p));

    37	/**
    38	 * The confirm stage's gate. The protocol must be frozen, its hash must equal the recorded freeze hash, and BOTH files must be
    39	 * committed (HEAD blobs) byte-equal to the working-tree files: a working-tree-only freeze is not a freeze (PLAN.md, section 7).
    40	 * `committed` holds the HEAD blob texts (null when the file is not in HEAD). Returns the refusal, or null.
    41	 */
    42	export function freezeRefusal(p: Protocol, freezeText: string | null): string | null {
    43	  if (!p.frozen || p.minEffect === null || p.devPrior === null) return "protocol.json is not frozen (run the dev stage, then freeze)";
    44	  if (freezeText === null) return "protocol.freeze.sha256 is missing";
    45	  if (freezeText.trim() !== protocolHash(p)) return `protocol hash ${protocolHash(p)} does not match the frozen ${freezeText.trim()}`;
    46	  return null;
    47	}
    48	export interface CommittedCopies {
    49	  protocol: string | null;
    50	  freeze: string | null;
    51	  protocolWorking: string;
    52	  freezeWorking: string;
    53	}
    54	export function committedRefusal(c: CommittedCopies): string | null {
    55	  if (c.protocol === null) return "protocol.json is not committed (HEAD has no copy): commit the freeze before confirming";
    56	  if (c.freeze === null) return "protocol.freeze.sha256 is not committed (HEAD has no copy): commit the freeze before confirming";
    57	  if (c.protocol !== c.protocolWorking) return "protocol.json differs from its committed copy: commit the freeze before confirming";
    58	  if (c.freeze !== c.freezeWorking) return "protocol.freeze.sha256 differs from its committed copy: commit the freeze before confirming";
    59	  return null;
    60	}
    61	export const confirmRefusal = (p: Protocol, freezeText: string | null, committed: CommittedCopies | undefined): string | null =>
    62	  freezeRefusal(p, freezeText) ?? (committed ? committedRefusal(committed) : "no committed copy supplied: the freeze must be committed before test outcomes are generated");
    63	export const splitPlanOf = (p: Protocol): SplitPlan => assignSplits(p.families.map((f) => f.id), p.salt, p.nDev, p.nQualify);

   111	export interface Generated {
   112	  results: FamilyResult[];
   113	  /** Seed-variant results for the qualify families (stability). */
   114	  variants: FamilyResult[];
   115	}
   116	
   117	export function generate(p: Protocol, familyIds: readonly string[], includeVariants: boolean): Generated {
   118	  const byId = new Map(p.families.map((f) => [f.id, f]));
   119	  const plan = splitPlanOf(p);
   120	  const results: FamilyResult[] = [];
   121	  const variants: FamilyResult[] = [];
   122	  // Budget first: bounds and planned cell-steps are checked for every run (variants included) before anything is simulated.
   123	  const planned = plannedCellSteps(p);
   124	  if (planned > p.caps.maxCellStepsPerFamily) throw new Error(`a family run plans ${planned} cell-steps, over the cap ${p.caps.maxCellStepsPerFamily}`);
   125	  for (const id of familyIds) if (!byId.has(id)) throw new Error(`unknown family ${id}`);
   126	  for (const id of familyIds) {
   127	    const fam = byId.get(id)!;
   128	    results.push(runFamily(p, fam));
   129	    if (includeVariants && plan.qualify.includes(id)) variants.push(runFamily(p, fam, fam.seed + p.seedVariantOffset));
   130	  }
   131	  return { results, variants };
   132	}

   134	export interface CaseRow {
   135	  caseId: string;
   136	  familyId: string;
   137	  split: Split;
   138	  mechanismId: string;
   139	  expected: Direction3;
   140	  envelopeHash: string;
   141	  effect: { bodyMass: number; livingCells: number; freeNutrient: number };
   142	  before: number;
   143	  treated: number;
   144	  control: number;
   145	  treatedHash: string;
   146	}
   147	
   148	export function caseRows(p: Protocol, g: readonly FamilyResult[]): CaseRow[] {
   149	  const plan = splitPlanOf(p);
   150	  const rows: CaseRow[] = [];
   151	  for (const r of g) {
   152	    const split: Split = plan.dev.includes(r.familyId) ? "dev" : "test";
   153	    for (const a of r.arms) {
   154	      const env = r.envelopes.find((e) => e.caseId === a.caseId)!;
   155	      const m = p.mechanisms.find((x) => x.id === a.mechanismId)!;
   156	      rows.push({
   157	        caseId: a.caseId,
   158	        familyId: r.familyId,
   159	        split,
   160	        mechanismId: a.mechanismId,
   161	        expected: m.expected,
   162	        envelopeHash: env.envelopeHash,
   163	        effect: { bodyMass: a.effect.bodyMass, livingCells: a.effect.livingCells, freeNutrient: a.effect.freeNutrient },
   164	        before: env.census[env.census.length - 1].mass,
   165	        treated: a.treated.bodyMass,
   166	        control: a.control.bodyMass,
   167	        treatedHash: a.treatedHash,
   168	      });
   169	    }
   170	  }
   171	  return rows;
   172	}
   173	
   174	/** Prespecified: a label that changes when the threshold is scaled by 3/4 or 5/4 is measurement-limited (kept in every denominator). */
   175	export function measurementLimited(effect: number, minEffect: number): boolean {
   176	  const l = labelOf(effect, minEffect);
   177	  return labelOf(effect, minEffect * 0.75) !== l || labelOf(effect, minEffect * 1.25) !== l;
   178	}
   179	
   180	/**
   181	 * The dev qualification report. Tuning (the minEffect candidates' tallies, cross-seed stability) uses ONLY the reserved `qualify`
   182	 * subset of dev families; `devEffects` carries every dev family's effects separately, for the dev class prior alone.
   183	 */
   184	export function qualificationReport(p: Protocol, dev: Generated) {
   185	  const plan = splitPlanOf(p);
   186	  const allRows = caseRows(p, dev.results);
   187	  const rows = allRows.filter((r) => plan.qualify.includes(r.familyId));
   188	  const tallies = p.minEffectCandidates.map((t) => {
   189	    const perMech = p.mechanisms.map((m) => {
   190	      const rs = rows.filter((r) => r.mechanismId === m.id);
   191	      const count = { up: 0, down: 0, none: 0 };
   192	      for (const r of rs) count[labelOf(r.effect.bodyMass, t)]++;
   193	      return { mechanism: m.id, expected: m.expected, ...count, agreeWithExpected: count[m.expected] };
   194	    });
   195	    return { minEffect: t, perMechanism: perMech };
   196	  });
   197	  const variantRows = caseRows(p, dev.variants);
   198	  const stability = p.minEffectCandidates.map((t) => {
   199	    const a: Direction3[] = [], b: Direction3[] = [];
   200	    for (const v of variantRows) {
   201	      const base = rows.find((r) => r.caseId === v.caseId);
   202	      if (!base) continue;
   203	      a.push(labelOf(base.effect.bodyMass, t));
   204	      b.push(labelOf(v.effect.bodyMass, t));
   205	    }
   206	    return { minEffect: t, ...signAgreement(a, b) };
   207	  });
   208	  return {
   209	    protocolHash: protocolHash(p),
   210	    note: "measured labels are never relabelled to match expectations; disagreements are reported. Tuning fields (effects, variantEffects, tallies, crossSeedEffectStability) cover the qualify subset only; devEffects (all dev families) feed the dev class prior only.",
   211	    qualifyFamilies: plan.qualify,
   212	    effects: rows.map((r) => ({ caseId: r.caseId, familyId: r.familyId, mechanism: r.mechanismId, bodyMass: r.effect.bodyMass, livingCells: r.effect.livingCells, freeNutrient: r.effect.freeNutrient })),
   213	    devEffects: allRows.map((r) => ({ caseId: r.caseId, familyId: r.familyId, mechanism: r.mechanismId, bodyMass: r.effect.bodyMass })),
   214	    variantEffects: variantRows.map((r) => ({ caseId: r.caseId, mechanism: r.mechanismId, bodyMass: r.effect.bodyMass })),
   215	    tallies,
   216	    crossSeedEffectStability: stability,
   217	    neutralityChecks: dev.results.reduce((s, r) => s + r.neutralityChecks, 0),
   218	    cellSteps: dev.results.reduce((s, r) => s + r.cellSteps, 0) + dev.variants.reduce((s, r) => s + r.cellSteps, 0),
   219	  };
   220	}
   221	
   222	/** Class frequencies of a list of effects at a threshold, as per-mille summing to exactly 1000 (largest remainder; ties to the earlier of up, down, none). */
   223	export function priorFromEffects(effects: readonly number[], minEffect: number): Probs {
   224	  const c = { up: 0, down: 0, none: 0 };
   225	  for (const e of effects) c[labelOf(e, minEffect)]++;
   226	  const n = effects.length;
   227	  if (n === 0) throw new Error("no dev effects");
   228	  const raw = CLASSES.map((k) => (c[k] * 1000) / n);
   229	  const fl = raw.map(Math.floor);
   230	  let left = 1000 - fl.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
   231	  const order = raw.map((r, i) => ({ i, f: r - fl[i] })).sort((a, b) => b.f - a.f || a.i - b.i);
   232	  for (const o of order) {
   233	    if (left <= 0) break;
   234	    fl[o.i]++;
   235	    left--;
   236	  }
   237	  return { up: fl[0], down: fl[1], none: fl[2] };
   238	}
   239	export const devPriorAt = (p: Protocol, dev: Generated, minEffect: number): Probs => priorFromEffects(caseRows(p, dev.results).map((r) => r.effect.bodyMass), minEffect);

   241	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
   242	// Proposers and evaluation.
   243	
   244	export function buildProposers(p: Protocol, envelopes: readonly PredictionEnvelope[]): Proposer[] {
   245	  if (!p.devPrior) throw new Error("protocol has no devPrior: freeze it first");
   246	  const plan = splitPlanOf(p);
   247	  const splitOf = (f: string): Split | undefined => (plan.dev.includes(f) ? "dev" : plan.test.includes(f) ? "test" : undefined);
   248	  const first = new Map<string, PredictionEnvelope>();
   249	  for (const e of envelopes) if (!first.has(e.familyId)) first.set(e.familyId, e);
   250	  return [
   251	    priorMajority(p.devPrior),
   252	    alwaysNone(),
   253	    fixedRule(p.proposers.fixedRule),
   254	    numericTrend(p.proposers.numericTrend),
   255	    v0Rules(p.proposers.v0Rules),
   256	    shuffledEvidence(p.proposers.numericTrend, p.donors, (f) => first.get(f), splitOf),
   257	    abstainAll(),
   258	    mechanismRule(p.proposers.mechanismRule),
   259	  ];
   260	}
38:export function canonical(v: unknown, path = "$"): string {
60:export const bodySha256 = (body: ProposalBody): string => sha(canonical(body));
63:export const specHash = (spec: RunSpec): string => sha(canonical(spec));
600:export function canonicalOrder(list: readonly ScheduledIntervention[]): ScheduledIntervention[] {
     1	// Deterministic baseline proposers (PLAN.md, section 4). A proposer takes only an envelope and returns a prediction (never
     2	// outcomes, never another case's task). Constants are frozen in protocol.json and passed in; nothing here reads a file or a clock.
     3	import { RULES, type Prediction as RulesPrediction } from "../propose.ts";
     4	import { PREDICTION_VERSION, taskKey, type CensusPoint, type Direction3, type PredictionEnvelope } from "./envelope.ts";
     5	import { donorAllowed, type Split } from "./splits.ts";
     6	import type { Prediction, Probs } from "./validate.ts";
     7	
     8	export interface Proposer {
     9	  id: string;
    10	  /** baseline: a rule a model has to beat; control: a deliberately uninformed run; oracle: knows the mechanism, a dev diagnostic only. */
    11	  role: "baseline" | "control" | "oracle";
    12	  propose(env: PredictionEnvelope): Prediction;
    13	}
    14	
    15	export interface ProposerParams {
    16	  fixedRule: { lead: number };
    17	  numericTrend: { window: number; minRelPerMille: number; lead: number; mid: number; low: number };
    18	  v0Rules: { lead: number };
    19	  mechanismRule: { lead: number; lowDoseQuanta: number; scarceNutrient: number };
    20	}
    21	
    22	export function predictionOf(env: PredictionEnvelope, probs: Probs | null, reason: string): Prediction {
    23	  const base = { version: PREDICTION_VERSION, caseId: env.caseId, envelopeHash: env.envelopeHash, task: env.task, reason };
    24	  return probs ? { ...base, probs, abstain: false } : { ...base, abstain: true };
    25	}
    26	
    27	/** `lead` per-mille on `dir`, the rest split evenly between the other two (the first, in order up/down/none, takes any odd remainder). */
    28	export function leadProbs(dir: Direction3, lead: number): Probs {
    29	  const rest = 1000 - lead;
    30	  const half = Math.floor(rest / 2);
    31	  const out: Probs = { up: 0, down: 0, none: 0 };
    32	  const others = (["up", "down", "none"] as const).filter((c) => c !== dir);
    33	  out[dir] = lead;
    34	  out[others[0]] = rest - half;
    35	  out[others[1]] = half;
    36	  return out;
    37	}
    38	
    39	/** Largest-remainder rounding of non-negative weights to per-mille summing to exactly 1000. */
    40	export function toPerMille(w: Record<Direction3, number>): Probs {
    41	  const tot = w.up + w.down + w.none;
    42	  if (!(tot > 0)) throw new Error("weights must have a positive sum");
    43	  const cls = ["up", "down", "none"] as const;
    44	  const raw = cls.map((c) => (w[c] * 1000) / tot);
    45	  const fl = raw.map(Math.floor);
    46	  let left = 1000 - fl.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    47	  const order = raw.map((r, i) => ({ i, f: r - fl[i] })).sort((a, b) => b.f - a.f || a.i - b.i);
    48	  for (const o of order) {
    49	    if (left <= 0) break;
    50	    fl[o.i]++;
    51	    left--;
    52	  }
    53	  return { up: fl[0], down: fl[1], none: fl[2] };
    54	}
    55	
    56	export const fixedRule = (k: ProposerParams["fixedRule"]): Proposer => ({
    57	  id: "fixedRule",
    58	  role: "baseline",
    59	  propose(env) {
    60	    const kind = env.task.op.kind;
    61	    const dir: Direction3 = kind === "feed" ? "up" : kind === "lesion" || kind === "drain" ? "down" : "none";
    62	    return predictionOf(env, leadProbs(dir, k.lead), `fixed rule: ${kind} -> ${dir}`);
    63	  },
    64	});
    65	
    66	/** The census-only trend: slope of the primary readout over the last `window` samples, extrapolated over the horizon, as a share of the current level. Ignores frames and the task. */
    67	export function trendDirection(census: readonly CensusPoint[], horizon: number, k: ProposerParams["numericTrend"]): Direction3 {
    68	  if (census.length < k.window + 1) return "none";
    69	  const last = census[census.length - 1], first = census[census.length - 1 - k.window];
    70	  const span = last.step - first.step;
    71	  if (span <= 0) return "none";
    72	  // change over the horizon, per-mille of the current level, in integer arithmetic.
    73	  const delta = (last.mass - first.mass) * horizon * 1000;
    74	  const bar = k.minRelPerMille * Math.max(last.mass, 1) * span;
    75	  return delta >= bar ? "up" : delta <= -bar ? "down" : "none";
    76	}
    77	function trendProbs(dir: Direction3, k: ProposerParams["numericTrend"]): Probs {
    78	  if (dir === "none") return { up: k.low, down: k.low, none: 1000 - 2 * k.low };
    79	  const other: Direction3 = dir === "up" ? "down" : "up";
    80	  const out: Probs = { up: 0, down: 0, none: k.mid };
    81	  out[dir] = k.lead;
    82	  out[other] = 1000 - k.lead - k.mid;
    83	  return out;
    84	}
    85	export const numericTrend = (k: ProposerParams["numericTrend"]): Proposer => ({
    86	  id: "numericTrend",
    87	  role: "baseline",
    88	  propose(env) {
    89	    const d = trendDirection(env.census, env.task.horizon, k);
    90	    return predictionOf(env, trendProbs(d, k), `census trend -> ${d}`);
    91	  },
    92	});
    93	
    94	/**
    95	 * The shuffled-evidence control: the numeric-trend rule run on a donor family's census. The donor is assigned by the frozen
    96	 * derangement; a donor from the same family, the same history (a later window of it) or another split is refused (throws), so a
    97	 * control can never be handed the case's own future.
    98	 */
    99	export function shuffledEvidence(
   100	  k: ProposerParams["numericTrend"],
   101	  donors: Readonly<Record<string, string>>,
   102	  envelopeOf: (familyId: string) => PredictionEnvelope | undefined,
   103	  splitOf: (familyId: string) => Split | undefined,
   104	): Proposer {
   105	  return {
   106	    id: "shuffledEvidence",
   107	    role: "control",
   108	    propose(env) {
   109	      const donorFamily = donors[env.familyId];
   110	      const donor = donorFamily === undefined ? undefined : envelopeOf(donorFamily);
   111	      if (!donor) throw new Error(`no donor envelope for family ${env.familyId}`);
   112	      const ok = donorAllowed(env, donor, splitOf);
   113	      if (!ok.ok) throw new Error(`donor refused for ${env.caseId}: ${ok.reason}`);
   114	      const d = trendDirection(donor.census, env.task.horizon, k);
   115	      return predictionOf(env, trendProbs(d, k), `donor census trend -> ${d}`);
   116	    },
   117	  };
   118	}
   119	
   120	export const priorMajority = (devPrior: Probs): Proposer => ({
   121	  id: "priorMajority",
   122	  role: "baseline",
   123	  propose: (env) => predictionOf(env, { ...devPrior }, "dev-split class frequencies"),
   124	});
   125	export const alwaysNone = (): Proposer => ({ id: "alwaysNone", role: "baseline", propose: (env) => predictionOf(env, { up: 0, down: 0, none: 1000 }, "always none") });
   126	export const abstainAll = (): Proposer => ({ id: "abstainAll", role: "baseline", propose: (env) => predictionOf(env, null, "abstains on every case") });
   127	
   128	/** The v0 rule proposer's directional/replay prediction mapped into the schema: 600 on the stated direction, the remainder on none (explicit, versioned). */
   129	export const RULES_MAP_VERSION = 1;
   130	export function fromRulesProposal(env: PredictionEnvelope, pred: RulesPrediction, lead: number): Prediction {
   131	  const probs: Probs = pred.kind === "directional" && pred.direction ? { up: 0, down: 0, none: 1000 - lead, [pred.direction]: lead } as Probs : { up: 0, down: 0, none: 1000 };
   132	  return predictionOf(env, probs, `rules-v0 (map v${RULES_MAP_VERSION}): ${pred.kind}${pred.direction ? " " + pred.direction : ""}`);
   133	}
   134	export const v0Rules = (k: ProposerParams["v0Rules"]): Proposer => ({
   135	  id: "v0Rules",
   136	  role: "baseline",
   137	  propose(env) {
   138	    // The v0 rule table (tools/lib/propose.ts RULES): feed and drain rows from the nutrient-held signals, lesion from the standing-body signals, control otherwise.
   139	    const kind = env.task.op.kind;
   140	    const row = kind === "feed" ? RULES.livingCells.low : kind === "drain" ? RULES.livingCells.high : kind === "lesion" ? RULES.lineages.high : RULES.lineages.low;
   141	    const pred: RulesPrediction = row.action === "control" ? { kind: "replay", statement: "replays" } : { kind: "directional", statement: `${row.action}`, direction: row.direction };
   142	    return fromRulesProposal(env, pred, k.lead);
   143	  },
   144	});
   145	
   146	/** Positive control, a dev diagnostic only: knows the fixture mechanisms (lesion centre occupancy from the frames, dose against the nutrient in the field). Never a baseline to beat. */
   147	export const mechanismRule = (k: ProposerParams["mechanismRule"]): Proposer => ({
   148	  id: "mechanismRule",
   149	  role: "oracle",
   150	  propose(env) {
   151	    const op = env.task.op;
   152	    const last = env.frames[env.frames.length - 1];
   153	    const cen = env.census[env.census.length - 1];
   154	    const block = (x: number, y: number): number => {
   155	      const w = env.config.tileW * env.config.tilesX;
   156	      const bs = Math.floor(w / last.side);
   157	      return last.mass[Math.floor(y / bs) * last.side + Math.floor(x / bs)];
   158	    };
   159	    let dir: Direction3 = "none";
   160	    if (op.kind === "lesion") dir = block(op.x!, op.y!) > 0 ? "down" : "none";
   161	    else if (op.kind === "feed") dir = (op.r! * op.r! * 3 * op.amount!) >= k.lowDoseQuanta ? "up" : "none";
   162	    else if (op.kind === "drain") dir = cen.freeNutrient >= k.scarceNutrient ? "down" : "none";
   163	    return predictionOf(env, leadProbs(dir, k.lead), `mechanism: ${op.kind} -> ${dir}`);
   164	  },
   165	});
   166	
   167	export const caseKey = (env: PredictionEnvelope): string => taskKey(env.task);

    19	export interface Adapted {
    20	  prediction: Prediction | null;
    21	  validation: Validation;
    22	}

    24	/** Raw output (a JSON string or an object) through the validator. An invalid output yields no prediction and its errors. */
    25	export function adaptModelOutput(raw: unknown, env: PredictionEnvelope): Adapted {
    26	  let value = raw;
    27	  if (typeof raw === "string") {
    28	    try {
    29	      value = JSON.parse(raw);
    30	    } catch {
    31	      return { prediction: null, validation: { ok: false, errors: ["output is not JSON"] } };
    32	    }
    33	  }
    34	  const validation = validatePrediction(value, env);
    35	  return { prediction: validation.ok ? (value as Prediction) : null, validation };
    36	}

   262	export const BASELINE_IDS = ["priorMajority", "alwaysNone", "fixedRule", "numericTrend", "v0Rules"] as const;

   264	export interface PredictionLine {
   265	  proposer: string;
   266	  prediction: Prediction;
   267	}
   268	
   269	/** `eligible` decides which envelopes a proposer runs on (the dev-only oracle is never run on, serialised for, or shown on test cases). */
   270	export function runProposers(proposers: readonly Proposer[], envelopes: readonly PredictionEnvelope[], eligible: (pr: Proposer, env: PredictionEnvelope) => boolean = () => true): PredictionLine[] {
   271	  const out: PredictionLine[] = [];
   272	  for (const pr of proposers) for (const e of envelopes) if (eligible(pr, e)) out.push({ proposer: pr.id, prediction: pr.propose(structuredClone(e)) });
   273	  return out;
   274	}
   275	/** Proposer ids that are dev-only oracles (a file of theirs is validated against dev envelopes only). */
   276	export const ORACLE_IDS: readonly string[] = ["mechanismRule"];
   277	/** The standard eligibility: oracle proposers only on dev-split envelopes. */
   278	export const devOnlyOracle = (p: Protocol) => {
   279	  const plan = splitPlanOf(p);
   280	  return (pr: Proposer, env: PredictionEnvelope): boolean => pr.role !== "oracle" || plan.dev.includes(env.familyId);
   281	};
   282	
   283	export function scoredCases(lines: readonly PredictionLine[], proposerId: string, envelopes: readonly PredictionEnvelope[], rows: readonly CaseRow[], minEffect: number): ScoredCase[] {
   284	  const mine = lines.filter((l) => l.proposer === proposerId).map((l) => JSON.stringify(l.prediction));
   285	  const check = validatePredictionFile(mine, envelopes);
   286	  const byRow = new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.caseId, r]));
   287	  const out: ScoredCase[] = [];
   288	  for (const e of envelopes) {
   289	    const row = byRow.get(e.caseId);
   290	    if (!row) continue;
   291	    const got = check.byCase.get(e.caseId);
   292	    const prediction = got?.prediction ?? null;
   293	    const status: ScoredCase["status"] = !got ? "missing" : !prediction ? "invalid" : prediction.abstain ? "abstained" : "answered";
   294	    out.push({ caseId: e.caseId, familyId: e.familyId, outcome: labelOf(row.effect.bodyMass, minEffect), prediction: status === "answered" ? prediction : null, status });
   295	  }
   296	  return out;
   297	}
