   299	export interface TableRow extends ProposerScore {
   300	  role: string;
   301	  brierAtMatchedCoverage: number | null;
   302	  accuracyAtMatchedCoverage: number | null;
   303	}
   304	export interface SplitTable {
   305	  split: string;
   306	  nCases: number;
   307	  nFamilies: number;
   308	  classCounts: Record<Direction3, number>;
   309	  meanAbsEffect: Record<string, number | null>;
   310	  rows: TableRow[];
   311	}
   312	
   313	export function tableFor(p: Protocol, name: string, proposers: readonly Proposer[], lines: readonly PredictionLine[], envelopes: readonly PredictionEnvelope[], rows: readonly CaseRow[], pick: (r: CaseRow) => boolean): SplitTable {
   314	  const sel = rows.filter(pick);
   315	  const ids = new Set(sel.map((r) => r.caseId));
   316	  const envs = envelopes.filter((e) => ids.has(e.caseId));
   317	  const prior = p.devPrior!;
   318	  const minEffect = p.minEffect!;
   319	  const byP: Record<string, ScoredCase[]> = {};
   320	  for (const pr of proposers) byP[pr.id] = scoredCases(lines, pr.id, envs, sel, minEffect);
   321	  // The coverage floor (a proposer that answers nothing) is left out of the matched set, else it would empty it.
   322	  const answering = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(byP).filter(([, cs]) => cs.some((c) => c.status === "answered")));
   323	  const matched = matchedCoverageSet(answering);
   324	  const classCounts: Record<Direction3, number> = { up: 0, down: 0, none: 0 };
   325	  for (const r of sel) classCounts[labelOf(r.effect.bodyMass, minEffect)]++;
   326	  const mean = (f: (r: CaseRow) => number): number | null => (sel.length ? sel.reduce((s, r) => s + Math.abs(f(r)), 0) / sel.length : null);
   327	  return {
   328	    split: name,
   329	    nCases: sel.length,
   330	    nFamilies: new Set(sel.map((r) => r.familyId)).size,
   331	    classCounts,
   332	    meanAbsEffect: { [`bodyMass (${READOUT_UNITS.bodyMass})`]: mean((r) => r.effect.bodyMass), [`livingCells (${READOUT_UNITS.livingCells}, diagnostic)`]: mean((r) => r.effect.livingCells), [`freeNutrient (${READOUT_UNITS.freeNutrient}, diagnostic)`]: mean((r) => r.effect.freeNutrient) },
   333	    rows: proposers.map((pr) => {
   334	      const s = scoreProposer(pr.id, byP[pr.id], prior);
   335	      const m = matched.size ? scoreProposer(pr.id, byP[pr.id], prior, matched) : null;
   336	      return { ...s, role: pr.role, brierAtMatchedCoverage: m?.brierAnswered ?? null, accuracyAtMatchedCoverage: m?.directionalAccuracy ?? null };
   337	    }),
   338	  };
   339	}

   341	export interface Comparison {
   342	  protocolHash: string;
   343	  noModelStatement: string;
   344	  strongestBaselineOnDev: string;
   345	  dev: SplitTable;
   346	  test: SplitTable;
   347	  testExcludingMeasurementLimited: SplitTable;
   348	  measurementLimitedCases: number;
   349	  perFamily: Record<string, { intervals: boolean; brierImputed: Record<string, Interval | null>; pairedVsStrongest: Record<string, Interval | null> }>;
   350	  /** Per proposer, over every envelope it was run on: file-level diagnostics that the per-split tables cannot carry. */
   351	  fileDiagnostics: Record<string, { lines: number; invalid: number; missing: number; extra: number; duplicates: number; notObject: number; badEnvelopes: number }>;
   352	  /** Per proposer: the predicted-argmax class counts over the test cases it answered; a single-class row means it cannot separate anything. */
   353	  argmaxTest: Record<string, Record<Direction3, number>>;
   354	  mechanismAudit: { mechanism: string; expected: Direction3; counts: Record<Direction3, number> }[];
   355	  trendConfound: TrendConfound;
   356	  validNonNullYieldAndCost: { totalCellSteps: number; costPerUseful: number | null };
   357	  crossSeedEffectStability: { n: number; agree: number; rate: number | null };
   358	  notes: string[];
   359	}
   360	
   361	export interface TrendConfound {
   362	  description: string;
   363	  pairedAlwaysRisingAccuracy: number | null;
   364	  naiveBeforeAfterAlwaysRisingAccuracy: number | null;
   365	  sham: { n: number; naiveUp: number; pairedNone: number };
   366	  lowDose: { n: number; naiveUp: number; pairedNone: number };
   367	  /** Cases whose treated value is above the fork value, of all cases: when 0, the fixtures cannot show the confound. */
   368	  risingCases: number;
   369	  totalCases: number;
   370	}
   371	/** A naive predictor that says "up" whenever treated ends above its before-value, scored by the naive before/after rule vs the paired rule. */
   372	export function trendConfoundOf(rows: readonly CaseRow[], minEffect: number): TrendConfound {
   373	  const share = (f: (r: CaseRow) => Direction3): number | null => (rows.length ? rows.filter((r) => f(r) === "up").length / rows.length : null);
   374	  const naive = (r: CaseRow): Direction3 => labelOf(r.treated - r.before, minEffect);
   375	  const paired = (r: CaseRow): Direction3 => labelOf(r.effect.bodyMass, minEffect);
   376	  const tally = (id: string) => {
   377	    const rs = rows.filter((r) => r.mechanismId === id);
   378	    return { n: rs.length, naiveUp: rs.filter((r) => naive(r) === "up").length, pairedNone: rs.filter((r) => paired(r) === "none").length };
   379	  };
   380	  return {
   381	    description: "Share of cases labelled up by the paired rule (treated minus control) vs the naive before/after rule (treated minus value at the fork); an always-rising predictor is right exactly when its rule says up.",
   382	    pairedAlwaysRisingAccuracy: share(paired),
   383	    naiveBeforeAfterAlwaysRisingAccuracy: share(naive),
   384	    sham: tally("sham"),
   385	    lowDose: tally("lowDoseFeed"),
   386	    risingCases: rows.filter((r) => r.treated > r.before).length,
   387	    totalCases: rows.length,
   388	  };
   389	}

   391	export function compare(p: Protocol, gen: Generated, envelopes: readonly PredictionEnvelope[], lines: readonly PredictionLine[], proposers: readonly Proposer[], eligible: (pr: Proposer, env: PredictionEnvelope) => boolean = devOnlyOracle(p)): Comparison {
   392	  const rows = caseRows(p, gen.results);
   393	  const minEffect = p.minEffect!;
   394	  const dev = tableFor(p, "dev", proposers, lines, envelopes, rows, (r) => r.split === "dev");
   395	  const noOracle = proposers.filter((x) => x.role !== "oracle");
   396	  const test = tableFor(p, "test", noOracle, lines, envelopes, rows, (r) => r.split === "test");
   397	  const limited = new Set(rows.filter((r) => measurementLimited(r.effect.bodyMass, minEffect)).map((r) => r.caseId));
   398	  const testNL = tableFor(p, "test, measurement-limited cases excluded", noOracle, lines, envelopes, rows, (r) => r.split === "test" && !limited.has(r.caseId));
   399	  const strongest = [...dev.rows].filter((r) => (BASELINE_IDS as readonly string[]).includes(r.id)).sort((a, b) => (a.brierImputed ?? 9) - (b.brierImputed ?? 9) || (a.id < b.id ? -1 : 1))[0].id;
   400	
   401	  // Per-family Brier (imputed) per proposer, intervals only with enough families.
   402	  const perFamily: Comparison["perFamily"] = {};
   403	  for (const split of ["dev", "test"] as const) {
   404	    const sel = rows.filter((r) => r.split === split);
   405	    const envs = envelopes.filter((e) => sel.some((r) => r.caseId === e.caseId));
   406	    const fam: Record<string, Map<string, number>> = {};
   407	    for (const pr of noOracle) {
   408	      const sc = scoredCases(lines, pr.id, envs, sel, minEffect);
   409	      fam[pr.id] = familyMeans(sc.map((c) => ({ familyId: c.familyId, v: c.status === "answered" ? brierOf(c.prediction!.probs!, c.outcome) : brierOf(p.devPrior!, c.outcome) })));
   410	    }
   411	    const enough = new Set(sel.map((r) => r.familyId)).size >= MIN_FAMILIES_FOR_INTERVALS;
   412	    perFamily[split] = {
   413	      intervals: enough,
   414	      brierImputed: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(fam).map(([id, m]) => [id, familyBootstrap(m, p.bootstrap.resamples, p.bootstrap.seed)])),
   415	      pairedVsStrongest: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(fam).filter(([id]) => id !== strongest).map(([id, m]) => [id, familyBootstrap(pairedFamilyDiff(m, fam[strongest]), p.bootstrap.resamples, p.bootstrap.seed)])),
   416	    };
   417	  }
   418	
   419	  const fileDiagnostics: Comparison["fileDiagnostics"] = {};
   420	  for (const pr of proposers) {
   421	    const mine = lines.filter((l) => l.proposer === pr.id).map((l) => JSON.stringify(l.prediction));
   422	    // Only the envelopes the proposer was meant to run on count as "missing" (the oracle is dev-only).
   423	    const meant = envelopes.filter((e) => eligible(pr, e));
   424	    const c = validatePredictionFile(mine, meant);
   425	    fileDiagnostics[pr.id] = { lines: mine.length, invalid: c.invalid, missing: c.missing.length, extra: c.extra.length, duplicates: c.duplicates.length, notObject: c.notObject, badEnvelopes: c.badEnvelopes.length };
   426	  }
   427	  const testIds = new Set(rows.filter((r) => r.split === "test").map((r) => r.caseId));
   428	  const argmaxTest: Comparison["argmaxTest"] = {};
   429	  for (const pr of noOracle) {
   430	    const counts: Record<Direction3, number> = { up: 0, down: 0, none: 0 };
   431	    const chk = validatePredictionFile(lines.filter((l) => l.proposer === pr.id).map((l) => JSON.stringify(l.prediction)), envelopes);
   432	    for (const [id, g] of chk.byCase) if (testIds.has(id) && g.prediction && !g.prediction.abstain) counts[argmax(g.prediction.probs!)]++;
   433	    argmaxTest[pr.id] = counts;
   434	  }
   435	  const degenerate = Object.entries(argmaxTest).filter(([, c]) => c.up + c.down + c.none > 0 && [c.up, c.down, c.none].filter((v) => v > 0).length === 1).map(([id, c]) => `${id} (all ${(["up", "down", "none"] as const).find((k) => c[k] > 0)})`);
   436	
   437	  const mechanismAudit = p.mechanisms.map((m) => {
   438	    const counts: Record<Direction3, number> = { up: 0, down: 0, none: 0 };
   439	    for (const r of rows.filter((x) => x.mechanismId === m.id)) counts[labelOf(r.effect.bodyMass, minEffect)]++;
   440	    return { mechanism: m.id, expected: m.expected, counts };
   441	  });
   442	
   443	  const totalCellSteps = gen.results.reduce((s, r) => s + r.cellSteps, 0) + gen.variants.reduce((s, r) => s + r.cellSteps, 0);
   444	  const vr = caseRows(p, gen.variants);
   445	  const stab = signAgreement(
   446	    vr.map((v) => labelOf(rows.find((r) => r.caseId === v.caseId)!.effect.bodyMass, minEffect)),
   447	    vr.map((v) => labelOf(v.effect.bodyMass, minEffect)),
   448	  );
   449	  return {
   450	    protocolHash: protocolHash(p),
   451	    noModelStatement: "No model call, no GPU, no paid compute: every prediction is from a deterministic rule. This table says nothing about any model.",
   452	    strongestBaselineOnDev: strongest,
   453	    dev,
   454	    test,
   455	    testExcludingMeasurementLimited: testNL,
   456	    measurementLimitedCases: limited.size,
   457	    perFamily,
   458	    fileDiagnostics,
   459	    argmaxTest,
   460	    mechanismAudit,
   461	    trendConfound: trendConfoundOf(rows, minEffect),
   462	    validNonNullYieldAndCost: { totalCellSteps, costPerUseful: costPerUseful(totalCellSteps, rows.map((r) => labelOf(r.effect.bodyMass, minEffect))) },
   463	    crossSeedEffectStability: stab,
   464	    notes: [
   465	      ...(["dev", "test"] as const).flatMap((sp) => (perFamily[sp].intervals ? [] : [`the ${sp} split has fewer than ${MIN_FAMILIES_FOR_INTERVALS} families: descriptive only (no intervals)`])),
   466	      "the strongest simple baseline is whichever of priorMajority, alwaysNone, fixedRule, numericTrend, v0Rules scores best on dev; a calibrated majority is legitimately strong",
   467	      "shuffledEvidence is a control, mechanismRule a dev-only oracle (run on dev cases only), abstainAll a coverage floor",
   468	      ...(degenerate.length ? [`degenerate on the test split, one predicted class for every answered case: ${degenerate.join(", ")}; a degenerate row cannot show the harness separating an evidence-using rule from its shuffled control (test: tools/test/prediction-proposers.test.ts, opposite-trend envelopes)`] : []),
   469	      ...(trendConfoundOf(rows, minEffect).risingCases === 0 ? ["no fixture case has a treated value above its fork value, so these fixtures cannot demonstrate the before/after trend confound (the confound rule is tested on a synthetic rising set)"] : []),
   470	    ],
   471	  };
   472	}

    30	export type Side = "high" | "low";
    31	export type Action = "feed" | "drain" | "lesion" | "control";

    34	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    35	// Canonical JSON and identity.
    36	
    37	/** Canonical JSON: keys sorted (UTF-16 order) at every depth, no whitespace, numbers as JSON.stringify writes them. Throws on a non-finite number, undefined, or a non-plain object. */
    38	export function canonical(v: unknown, path = "$"): string {
    39	  if (v === null || typeof v === "boolean" || typeof v === "string") return JSON.stringify(v);
    40	  if (typeof v === "number") {
    41	    if (!Number.isFinite(v)) throw new Error(`canonical: ${path} is not a finite number`);
    42	    return JSON.stringify(v);
    43	  }
    44	  if (Array.isArray(v)) return `[${v.map((x, i) => canonical(x, `${path}[${i}]`)).join(",")}]`;
    45	  if (typeof v === "object" && (Object.getPrototypeOf(v) === Object.prototype || Object.getPrototypeOf(v) === null)) {
    46	    const o = v as Record<string, unknown>;
    47	    return `{${Object.keys(o)
    48	      .sort()
    49	      .map((k) => {
    50	        if (o[k] === undefined) throw new Error(`canonical: ${path}.${k} is undefined`);
    51	        return `${JSON.stringify(k)}:${canonical(o[k], `${path}.${k}`)}`;
    52	      })
    53	      .join(",")}}`;
    54	  }
    55	  throw new Error(`canonical: ${path} has unsupported type ${typeof v}`);
    56	}
    57	const sha = (text: string): string => sha256HexJS(new TextEncoder().encode(text));
    58	/** SHA-256 hex of a text (additive export for tools/lib/prediction, which hashes its envelopes the same way). */
    59	export const sha256Text = sha;
    60	export const bodySha256 = (body: ProposalBody): string => sha(canonical(body));
    61	export const proposalId = (body: ProposalBody): string => bodySha256(body).slice(0, 16);
    62	/** The SHA-256 of a run spec's canonical JSON: two rings that propose the same run are one proposal (the note, "Which rings"). */
    63	export const specHash = (spec: RunSpec): string => sha(canonical(spec));
    64	
    65	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    66	// The notes file (tools/notebook.ts's .notes.json): only the fields the proposer uses, each checked.
    67	
    68	export interface Receipt {
     1	// Builds the canonical output files (PLAN.md, section 9) as strings, a pure function of the protocol and the generated results, so
     2	// two runs are byte-identical. Non-canonical metadata (git SHA, timing, hardware) is the CLI's, in meta.json.
     3	import { canonical, sha256Text } from "../propose.ts";
     4	import type { PredictionEnvelope } from "./envelope.ts";
     5	import { labelOf, recipeHash, recipeOf } from "./fixtures.ts";
     6	import { renderReport } from "./report-html.ts";
     7	import { BASELINE_IDS, buildProposers, caseRows, checkLeaks, compare, devOnlyOracle, measurementLimited, protocolHash, runProposers, splitPlanOf, type CaseRow, type Comparison, type Generated, type PredictionLine, type Protocol, type SplitTable } from "./run.ts";
     8	
     9	export const CANONICAL_FILES = ["envelopes.jsonl", "protocol.snapshot.json", "recipes.jsonl", "predictions.jsonl", "outcomes.jsonl", "comparison.json", "comparison.md", "report.html"] as const;
    10	
    11	/** Entries in sorted key order: the markdown must not depend on whether the comparison came fresh from memory or back from canonical JSON (sorted keys). */
    12	const ent = <T>(o: Record<string, T>): [string, T][] => Object.entries(o).sort((a, b) => (a[0] < b[0] ? -1 : a[0] > b[0] ? 1 : 0));
    13	const f3 = (v: number | null): string => (v === null ? "n/a" : v.toFixed(3));
    14	
    15	function tableMd(t: SplitTable): string {
    16	  const lines = [`### ${t.split} (${t.nCases} cases, ${t.nFamilies} families; up ${t.classCounts.up}, down ${t.classCounts.down}, none ${t.classCounts.none})`, "", "| proposer | role | answered | abstained | invalid | missing | coverage | Brier (imputed) | Brier (answered) | direction acc. | balanced acc. | acc. at matched coverage | valid non-null yield |", "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|"];
    17	  for (const r of t.rows) lines.push(`| ${r.id} | ${r.role} | ${r.answered} | ${r.abstained} | ${r.invalid} | ${r.missing} | ${f3(r.coverage)} | ${f3(r.brierImputed)} | ${f3(r.brierAnswered)} | ${f3(r.directionalAccuracy)} | ${f3(r.balancedAccuracy)} | ${f3(r.accuracyAtMatchedCoverage)} | ${f3(r.validNonNullYield)} |`);
    18	  lines.push("", `Mean absolute effect: ${ent(t.meanAbsEffect).map(([k, v]) => `${k} ${v === null ? "n/a" : v.toFixed(1)}`).join("; ")}.`, "");
    19	  return lines.join("\n");
    20	}
    21	
    22	export function comparisonMarkdown(c: Comparison, minEffect: number): string {
    23	  const out: string[] = ["# Prediction envelope v0: comparison", "", c.noModelStatement, "", `Protocol hash \`${c.protocolHash}\`. Primary readout: body mass (B+P); minimum effect ${minEffect} mass quanta. Strongest simple baseline on dev: **${c.strongestBaselineOnDev}**. Measurement-limited cases (label flips at 0.75x or 1.25x the threshold): ${c.measurementLimitedCases}, kept in every denominator of the first two tables.`, "", tableMd(c.dev), tableMd(c.test), tableMd(c.testExcludingMeasurementLimited)];
    24	  out.push("## Family-level intervals (imputed Brier, whole-family bootstrap)", "");
    25	  for (const [split, v] of ent(c.perFamily)) {
    26	    out.push(`- ${split}: ${v.intervals ? "intervals computed" : "descriptive only (fewer than 10 families)"}`);
    27	    if (v.intervals) {
    28	      for (const [id, iv] of ent(v.brierImputed)) if (iv) out.push(`  - ${id}: ${f3(iv.mean)} [${f3(iv.lo)}, ${f3(iv.hi)}]`);
    29	      for (const [id, iv] of ent(v.pairedVsStrongest)) if (iv) out.push(`  - ${id} minus ${c.strongestBaselineOnDev}: ${f3(iv.mean)} [${f3(iv.lo)}, ${f3(iv.hi)}]`);
    30	    }

// Spawned `deno run --no-lock` smoke test of the CLI: a tiny smoke run, then `validate` on its predictions, then the confirm refusals.
import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { afterAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { confirmRefusal, draftProtocol, protocolHash, splitPlanOf } from "../lib/prediction/run.ts";
import { donorsFor } from "../lib/prediction/splits.ts";
import { tinyProtocol } from "./prediction-helpers.ts";

const root = fileURLToPath(new URL("../..", import.meta.url));
const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "predict-cli-"));
afterAll(() => rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }));
const deno = (args: string[]) => spawnSync("deno", ["run", "--no-lock", "-A", "tools/predict.ts", ...args], { cwd: root, encoding: "utf8" });

describe("tools/predict.ts", () => {
  const p = tinyProtocol();
  p.families = p.families.slice(0, 4);
  p.nDev = 2;
  p.nQualify = 1;
  p.donors = donorsFor(splitPlanOf(p), p.salt);
  const protoPath = join(dir, "protocol.json");
  writeFileSync(protoPath, JSON.stringify(p));

  it("runs a tiny smoke stage, writes the canonical files, and validate accepts its predictions", () => {
    const r = deno(["run", "--stage", "smoke", "--protocol", protoPath, "--out", join(dir, "out")]);
    expect(r.status, r.stderr).toBe(0);
    const meta = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(dir, "out/meta.json"), "utf8"));
    expect(meta.status).toBe("SMOKE");
    expect(meta.statement).toMatch(/No model call/);
    const v = deno(["validate", join(dir, "out/predictions.jsonl")]);
    expect(v.status, v.stdout + v.stderr).toBe(0);
    expect(v.stdout).toMatch(/fixedRule: \d+ lines, invalid 0, missing 0/);
  }, 300_000);
  it("validate fails on a corrupted line", () => {
    const f = join(dir, "bad.jsonl");
    const lines = readFileSync(join(dir, "out/predictions.jsonl"), "utf8").trim().split("\n");
    writeFileSync(f, [lines[0].replace('"abstain":false', '"abstain":true'), ...lines.slice(1)].join("\n") + "\n");
    const v = deno(["validate", f, "--envelopes", join(dir, "out/envelopes.jsonl")]);
    expect(v.status).toBe(1);
  }, 120_000);
  it("refuses an unknown stage", () => {
    const u = deno(["run", "--stage", "bogus", "--out", join(dir, "x")]);
    expect(u.status).toBe(1);
    expect(u.stderr).toMatch(/unknown --stage/);
  }, 120_000);
  it("validate fails on an empty predictions file, reporting every case as missing", () => {
    const f = join(dir, "empty.jsonl");
    writeFileSync(f, "");
    const v = deno(["validate", f, "--envelopes", join(dir, "out/envelopes.jsonl")]);
    expect(v.status).toBe(1);
    expect(v.stdout).toMatch(/\(empty file\): 0 lines, invalid 0, missing [1-9]/);
  }, 120_000);
  it("validate fails on a stray case, and on a tampered envelope whose stale hash is left in place", () => {
    const lines = readFileSync(join(dir, "out/predictions.jsonl"), "utf8").trim().split("\n");
    const mine = lines.filter((l) => l.startsWith('{"prediction":') && l.includes('"proposer":"fixedRule"'));
    const stray = join(dir, "stray.jsonl");
    writeFileSync(stray, [...mine, mine[0].replace(/"caseId":"c[0-9a-f]+"/, '"caseId":"cunknown"')].join("\n") + "\n");
    const v = deno(["validate", stray, "--envelopes", join(dir, "out/envelopes.jsonl")]);
    expect(v.status).toBe(1);
    expect(v.stdout).toMatch(/extra 1/);
    const envs = readFileSync(join(dir, "out/envelopes.jsonl"), "utf8").trim().split("\n");
    const env0 = JSON.parse(envs[0]);
    env0.census[0].mass += 1; // content changed, stored envelopeHash left stale
    const tampered = join(dir, "tampered-envs.jsonl");
    writeFileSync(tampered, [JSON.stringify(env0), ...envs.slice(1)].join("\n") + "\n");
    const t = deno(["validate", join(dir, "out/predictions.jsonl"), "--envelopes", tampered]);
    expect(t.status).toBe(1);
    expect(t.stdout).toMatch(/unsound envelopes [1-9]/);
  }, 120_000);
  it("report re-renders the derived files byte for byte from the canonical data", () => {
    const before = ["comparison.md", "report.html", "canonical.sha256"].map((n) => readFileSync(join(dir, "out", n), "utf8"));
    const r = deno(["report", "--out", join(dir, "out")]);
    expect(r.status, r.stderr).toBe(0);
    const after = ["comparison.md", "report.html", "canonical.sha256"].map((n) => readFileSync(join(dir, "out", n), "utf8"));
    expect(after).toEqual(before);
  }, 120_000);
  it("the confirm gate refuses an unfrozen protocol, a missing freeze file, a hash mismatch and an uncommitted freeze", () => {
    const d = draftProtocol();
    expect(confirmRefusal(d, null, undefined)).toMatch(/not frozen/);
    const f = { ...d, frozen: true, minEffect: 50, devPrior: { up: 100, down: 100, none: 800 } };
    expect(confirmRefusal(f, null, undefined)).toMatch(/missing/);
    expect(confirmRefusal(f, "0".repeat(64), undefined)).toMatch(/does not match/);
    expect(confirmRefusal({ ...f, minEffect: 51 }, protocolHash(f), undefined)).toMatch(/does not match/);
    const h = protocolHash(f) + "\n", pj = JSON.stringify(f);
    // A matching working tree is not enough: both files must be in HEAD, byte-equal.
    expect(confirmRefusal(f, h, undefined)).toMatch(/committed/);
    expect(confirmRefusal(f, h, { protocol: null, freeze: null, protocolWorking: pj, freezeWorking: h })).toMatch(/protocol.json is not committed/);
    expect(confirmRefusal(f, h, { protocol: pj, freeze: null, protocolWorking: pj, freezeWorking: h })).toMatch(/freeze.sha256 is not committed/);
    expect(confirmRefusal(f, h, { protocol: pj + " ", freeze: h, protocolWorking: pj, freezeWorking: h })).toMatch(/differs from its committed/);
    expect(confirmRefusal(f, h, { protocol: pj, freeze: "x", protocolWorking: pj, freezeWorking: h })).toMatch(/differs from its committed/);
    expect(confirmRefusal(f, h, { protocol: pj, freeze: h, protocolWorking: pj, freezeWorking: h })).toBeNull();
  });
});

// The static notebook view, the outputs' byte reproducibility, and the comparison's shape.
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { PredictionEnvelope } from "../lib/prediction/envelope.ts";
import { sha256Text } from "../lib/propose.ts";
import { CANONICAL_FILES, buildOutputs, rerender } from "../lib/prediction/output.ts";
import { devOnlyOracle, generate, splitPlanOf, tableFor } from "../lib/prediction/run.ts";
import { abstainAll, alwaysNone, fixedRule } from "../lib/prediction/proposers.ts";

const T = 240_000;

describe("report.html", () => {
  const { p, built } = tinyPipeline();
  const html = built.files["report.html"];
  const envs = built.files["envelopes.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
  it("contains every case id, no scripts and no external URLs", () => {
    for (const e of envs) expect(html).toContain(`id="${e.caseId}"`);
    expect(html).not.toMatch(/<script/i);
    expect(html).not.toMatch(/https?:\/\//i);
    expect(html).not.toMatch(/\son\w+=/i);
  }, T);
  it("shows failed predictions first and escapes the free-text reason", () => {
    const rows = built.files["outcomes.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
    const lines = built.files["predictions.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
    const evil = lines.map((l: { proposer: string; prediction: { reason: string } }) => (l.proposer === "fixedRule" ? { ...l, prediction: { ...l.prediction, reason: "<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>" } } : l));
    const out = renderReport({ comparison: built.comparison, envelopes: envs, lines: evil as never, rows, minEffect: p.minEffect!, proposerIds: ["fixedRule", "numericTrend"] });
    expect(out).not.toContain("<img src=x");
    expect(out).toContain("&lt;img src=x");
    const first = out.match(/<article[\s\S]*?<\/article>/)![0];
    expect(first).toContain('<li class="bad">');
    const lastArticle = [...out.matchAll(/<article[\s\S]*?<\/article>/g)].pop()![0];
    const bads = (h: string) => (h.match(/<li class="bad">/g) ?? []).length;
    expect(bads(first)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(bads(lastArticle));
  }, T);
  it("lists invalid predictions in their own section", () => {
    const rows = built.files["outcomes.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
    const lines = built.files["predictions.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
    const broken = lines.map((l: { proposer: string; prediction: Record<string, unknown> }, i: number) => (l.proposer === "fixedRule" && i % 5 === 0 ? { ...l, prediction: { ...l.prediction, probs: { up: 1, down: 1, none: 1 } } } : l));
    const out = renderReport({ comparison: built.comparison, envelopes: envs, lines: broken as never, rows, minEffect: p.minEffect!, proposerIds: ["fixedRule"] });
    expect(out).toMatch(/Invalid or missing predictions<\/h2><ul>/);
  }, T);
});

describe("outputs", () => {
  const { p, built } = tinyPipeline();
  it("are byte-reproducible: a second generation gives identical canonical files", () => {
    const again = buildOutputs(p, generate(p, p.families.map((f) => f.id), true));
    for (const n of CANONICAL_FILES) expect(again.files[n], n).toBe(built.files[n]);
    expect(again.canonicalSha256).toBe(built.canonicalSha256);
  }, T);
  it("canonical.sha256 lists exactly the canonical files with their hashes", () => {
    const listed = built.canonicalSha256.trim().split("\n").map((l) => l.split("  "));
    expect(listed.map((x) => x[1])).toEqual([...CANONICAL_FILES]);
    for (const [h, n] of listed) expect(h).toBe(sha256Text(built.files[n]));
  });
  it("every proposer's file validates against the envelopes; shuffled never uses its own family; the oracle is absent from the test table", () => {
    const envs = built.files["envelopes.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
    const lines = built.files["predictions.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
    const ids = new Set(lines.map((l: { proposer: string }) => l.proposer));
    const plan = splitPlanOf(p);
    for (const id of ids) {
      const meant = id === "mechanismRule" ? envs.filter((e: { familyId: string }) => plan.dev.includes(e.familyId)) : envs;
      const r = validatePredictionFile(lines.filter((l: { proposer: string }) => l.proposer === id).map((l: { prediction: unknown }) => JSON.stringify(l.prediction)), meant);
      expect(r.invalid + r.missing.length + r.extra.length, id).toBe(0);
    }
    expect(built.comparison.test.rows.map((r) => r.id)).not.toContain("mechanismRule");
    expect(built.comparison.dev.rows.map((r) => r.id)).toContain("mechanismRule");
    expect(built.comparison.test.rows.find((r) => r.id === "abstainAll")!.coverage).toBe(0);
    expect(built.comparison.perFamily.test.intervals).toBe(false);
  });
  it("the dev-only oracle is run on, serialised for and shown on dev cases only", () => {
    const plan = splitPlanOf(p);
    const lines = built.files["predictions.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l) as { proposer: string; prediction: { caseId: string } });
    const envs = built.files["envelopes.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l) as { caseId: string; familyId: string });
    const fam = new Map(envs.map((e) => [e.caseId, e.familyId]));
    const oracle = lines.filter((l) => l.proposer === "mechanismRule");
    expect(oracle.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    for (const l of oracle) expect(plan.dev, l.prediction.caseId).toContain(fam.get(l.prediction.caseId));
    const html = built.files["report.html"];
    for (const e of envs.filter((x) => plan.test.includes(x.familyId))) {
      const art = html.match(new RegExp(`<article id="${e.caseId}"[\\s\\S]*?</article>`))![0];
      expect(art).not.toContain("mechanismRule");
    }
    expect(devOnlyOracle(p)({ id: "mechanismRule", role: "oracle", propose: () => null as never }, { familyId: plan.test[0] } as never)).toBe(false);
    expect(built.comparison.fileDiagnostics.mechanismRule.missing).toBe(0);
  });
  it("file-level diagnostics (extra, duplicate, invalid) reach the comparison, not just the per-case scores", () => {
    expect(Object.keys(built.comparison.fileDiagnostics)).toContain("fixedRule");
    expect(built.files["comparison.md"]).toContain("File-level diagnostics");
    expect(built.comparison.fileDiagnostics.fixedRule).toMatchObject({ invalid: 0, missing: 0, extra: 0, duplicates: 0, notObject: 0, badEnvelopes: 0 });
  });
  it("tableFor keeps the matched-coverage set non-empty when abstainAll is present (zero coverage is excluded from it)", () => {
    const envs = built.files["envelopes.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
    const rows = built.files["outcomes.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
    const ps = [fixedRule(p.proposers.fixedRule), alwaysNone(), abstainAll()];
    const lines = ps.flatMap((pr) => envs.map((e: PredictionEnvelope) => ({ proposer: pr.id, prediction: pr.propose(e) })));
    const t = tableFor(p, "t", ps, lines, envs, rows, () => true);
    for (const id of ["fixedRule", "alwaysNone"]) expect(t.rows.find((r) => r.id === id)!.accuracyAtMatchedCoverage, id).not.toBeNull();
    expect(t.rows.find((r) => r.id === "abstainAll")!.coverage).toBe(0);
  });
  it("a file with an extra unknown case and a duplicate is reported by validatePredictionFile, and the comparison would carry it", () => {
    const envs = built.files["envelopes.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
    const mine = built.files["predictions.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l)).filter((l: { proposer: string }) => l.proposer === "alwaysNone").map((l: { prediction: unknown }) => l.prediction as Record<string, unknown>);
    const lines = [...mine, { ...mine[0], caseId: "cunknown" }, mine[1]].map((x) => JSON.stringify(x));
    const r = validatePredictionFile(lines, envs);
    expect(r.extra).toEqual(["cunknown"]);
    expect(r.duplicates).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(r.invalid).toBe(2);
  });
  it("rerender reproduces the derived files byte for byte from the canonical data", () => {
    const again = rerender((n) => built.files[n]);
    for (const n of ["comparison.md", "report.html", "canonical.sha256"]) expect(again[n], n).toBe(built.files[n]);
  });
  it("abstainAll scores exactly the prior (imputation) on the headline Brier", () => {
    const prior = built.comparison.test.rows.find((r) => r.id === "priorMajority")!;
    const ab = built.comparison.test.rows.find((r) => r.id === "abstainAll")!;
    expect(ab.brierImputed).toBeCloseTo(prior.brierImputed!, 12);
  });
  it("the comparison states no model was run", () => {
    expect(built.comparison.noModelStatement).toMatch(/No model call/);
    expect(built.files["comparison.md"]).toContain("Trend-confound diagnostic");
  });
});
// Shared builders for the prediction-envelope tests: synthetic envelopes with no simulation, and a tiny protocol for the CPU fixtures.
import { defaultConfig } from "@bl/schema";
import { sealEnvelope, standardMenu, type CensusPoint, type Frame, type PredictionEnvelope, type TaskOp } from "../lib/prediction/envelope.ts";
import { draftProtocol, type Protocol } from "../lib/prediction/run.ts";

export function censusSeries(start: number, slope: number, n = 31, every = 10): CensusPoint[] {
  return Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => ({ step: i * every, livingCells: 100, freeNutrient: 50, mass: start + slope * i * every }));
}
export function frames(): Frame[] {
  const mass = new Array(64).fill(0);
  mass[0] = 5;
  return [{ step: 300, side: 8, mass }];
}
export function synthEnv(op: TaskOp, o: { familyId?: string; historyId?: string; caseId?: string; census?: CensusPoint[]; delta?: number; horizon?: number } = {}): PredictionEnvelope {
  const cfg = defaultConfig({ tileW: 32, tileH: 32, kernelRadius: 4, seed: 1 });
  return sealEnvelope({
    version: 1,
    caseId: o.caseId ?? "c-test",
    historyId: o.historyId ?? `${o.familyId ?? "fA"}:s1`,
    familyId: o.familyId ?? "fA",
    ruleVersion: 1,
    config: cfg,
    census: o.census ?? censusSeries(1000, 0),
    frames: frames(),
    menu: standardMenu(cfg, 60, 4096, 6),
    task: { subject: "world", op, delta: o.delta ?? 10, readout: "bodyMass", forkStep: 300, horizon: o.horizon ?? 200 },
  });
}
export const LESION: TaskOp = { kind: "lesion", x: 1, y: 1, r: 4 };
export const FEED: TaskOp = { kind: "feed", x: 1, y: 1, r: 4, amount: 64 };
export const DRAIN: TaskOp = { kind: "drain", x: 1, y: 1, r: 4, amount: 64 };
export const SHAM: TaskOp = { kind: "none" };

/** A tiny protocol: 6 short families, 2 mechanisms' worth of work per family, short windows. minEffect and devPrior are set. */
export function tinyProtocol(): Protocol {
  const p = draftProtocol();
  p.families = p.families.slice(0, 6);
  p.nDev = 3;
  p.nQualify = 2;
  p.window = { forkStep: 40, delta: 5, horizon: 30, censusEvery: 10, frameSteps: [20, 40] };
  p.mechanisms = p.mechanisms.filter((m) => ["feedOccupied", "lesionOccupied", "sham"].includes(m.id));
  p.proposers.numericTrend.window = 2;
  p.minEffect = 10;
  p.devPrior = { up: 200, down: 300, none: 500 };
  p.frozen = true;
  p.donors = {};
  return p;
}

import { buildOutputs, type Built } from "../lib/prediction/output.ts";
import { generate, splitPlanOf } from "../lib/prediction/run.ts";
import { donorsFor } from "../lib/prediction/splits.ts";

let cached: { p: Protocol; built: Built } | undefined;
/** A complete tiny pipeline (4 families, 3 mechanisms) built once per test file. */
export function tinyPipeline(): { p: Protocol; built: Built } {
  if (cached) return cached;
  const p = tinyProtocol();
  p.families = p.families.slice(0, 4);
  p.nDev = 2;
  p.nQualify = 1;
  p.donors = donorsFor(splitPlanOf(p), p.salt);
  const gen = generate(p, p.families.map((f) => f.id), true);
  cached = { p, built: buildOutputs(p, gen) };
  return cached;
}

// CPU fixtures (PLAN.md, section 6): paired replays on small worlds with short windows. Expectations come from the mechanisms and the
// invariants, not from tuning. Explicit per-test timeouts (the fixture runs take a few seconds).
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { census } from "@bl/metrics";
import { cellCount, CH, G, stateHash, totalsOf } from "@bl/schema";
import { RefSim } from "@bl/sim-ref";
import { CpuInterventionAdapter } from "../lib/prediction/adapter.ts";
import { assertNoPostFork, envelopeIntact, frameOf, hashEnvelopeBody, envelopeBody } from "../lib/prediction/envelope.ts";
import { assertFixtureBounds, checkBooks, heaviestCell, emptiestCell, labelOf, makeWorld, plannedCellSteps, runFamily } from "../lib/prediction/fixtures.ts";
import { freeNutrient, livingCells, totalMass } from "../lib/prediction/readouts.ts";
import { generate, trendConfoundOf, type CaseRow } from "../lib/prediction/run.ts";
import { tinyProtocol } from "./prediction-helpers.ts";

const T = 120_000;

describe("readouts", () => {
  it("livingCells equals the census's livingCells, freeNutrient and totalMass equal channel sums", () => {
    const p = tinyProtocol();
    const s = makeWorld(p, p.families[0]);
    const sim = new RefSim(s);
    sim.run(30);
    const n = cellCount(sim.cfg);
    const c = census({ step: sim.state.step, cfg: sim.cfg, cells: sim.state.cells, genomeHead: sim.state.genome.subarray(0, G.LIN_LO * n + n) } as never);
    expect(livingCells(sim.state)).toBe(c.livingCells);
    let a = 0, bp = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      a += sim.state.cells[CH.A * n + i];
      bp += sim.state.cells[CH.B * n + i] + sim.state.cells[CH.P * n + i];
    }
    expect(freeNutrient(sim.state)).toBe(a);
    expect(totalMass(sim.state)).toBe(bp);
  }, T);
});

describe("CPU intervention adapter", () => {
  const p = tinyProtocol();
  const s0 = makeWorld(p, p.families[0]);
  const start = totalsOf(s0.cfg, s0.cells);
  it("is independent of its source and of other arms", () => {
    const a = new CpuInterventionAdapter(s0, start), b = new CpuInterventionAdapter(s0, start);
    a.advanceTo(10);
    expect(s0.step).toBe(0);
    expect(b.state.step).toBe(0);
    expect(a.state.cells.buffer).not.toBe(b.state.cells.buffer);
    expect(a.state.cells.buffer).not.toBe(s0.cells.buffer);
    b.advanceTo(10);
    expect(stateHash(a.state)).toBe(stateHash(b.state));
  }, T);
  it("books energy and matter exactly across a lesion and a feed and a drain", () => {
    const a = new CpuInterventionAdapter(s0, start);
    a.advanceTo(10);
    const at = heaviestCell(a.state);
    a.apply({ step: 10, kind: "lesion", ...at, r: 3 });
    a.advanceTo(15);
    const fed = a.apply({ step: 15, kind: "feed", ...at, r: 3, amount: 20 });
    expect(fed.deliveredMatter).toBe(20 * 29);
    a.advanceTo(18);
    const drained = a.apply({ step: 18, kind: "feed", ...at, r: 3, amount: -5 });
    expect(drained.deliveredMatter).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0);
    a.advanceTo(30);
    expect(a.energyResidual()).toBe(0n);
    expect(a.matterResidual()).toBe(0n);
  }, T);
});

describe("one family", () => {
  const p = tinyProtocol();
  const fam = p.families[0];
  const r = runFamily(p, fam);
  it("builds envelopes with no post-fork data and an intact hash; frames are copies", () => {
    for (const e of r.envelopes) {
      expect(envelopeIntact(e)).toBe(true);
      expect(Math.max(...e.census.map((c) => c.step), ...e.frames.map((f) => f.step))).toBeLessThanOrEqual(e.task.forkStep);
      expect(e.menu.ops.map((o) => o.kind)).toEqual(["lesion", "feed", "drain", "none"]);
    }
    const body = envelopeBody(r.envelopes[0]);
    expect(hashEnvelopeBody({ ...body, census: body.census.map((c, i) => (i === 3 ? { ...c, mass: c.mass + 1 } : c)) })).not.toBe(r.envelopes[0].envelopeHash);
    expect(() => assertNoPostFork({ ...body, census: [...body.census, { step: body.task.forkStep + 1, livingCells: 0, freeNutrient: 0, mass: 0 }] })).toThrow(/after its fork step/);
    const sim = new RefSim(makeWorld(p, fam));
    const f = frameOf(sim.state);
    const before = JSON.stringify(f);
    sim.run(5);
    sim.state.cells.fill(7);
    expect(JSON.stringify(f)).toBe(before);
  }, T);
  it("sham equals control (hash), the envelope hash is stable across envelopes of a family's census, instrumented equals plain", () => {
    const sham = r.arms.find((a) => a.mechanismId === "sham")!;
    expect(sham.treatedHash).toBe(r.controlHash);
    expect(sham.effect.bodyMass).toBe(0);
    expect(r.neutralityChecks).toBeGreaterThan(3);
    // History to C and every arm's continuation through C+H are compared (the negative tests below prove the comparisons bite).
    expect(r.neutralityChecks).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1 + p.mechanisms.length);
    expect(r.cellSteps).toBe(plannedCellSteps(p));
    expect(new Set(r.envelopes.map((e) => JSON.stringify(e.census))).size).toBe(1);
  }, T);
  it("matter and energy books closed in every arm (runFamily throws otherwise); a lesion on the occupied centre lowers body mass, a feed raises it", () => {
    const les = r.arms.find((a) => a.mechanismId === "lesionOccupied")!;
    const feed = r.arms.find((a) => a.mechanismId === "feedOccupied")!;
    expect(les.energyResidual).toBe("0");
    expect(les.matterResidual).toBe("0");
    expect(feed.matterResidual).toBe("0");
    expect(les.effect.bodyMass).toBeLessThan(0);
    expect(feed.effect.bodyMass).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(feed.applied[0].deliveredMatter).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(les.applied[0].deliveredMatter).toBe(0);
  }, T);
  it("generation is deterministic: a rerun gives the same hashes", () => {
    const r2 = runFamily(p, fam);
    expect(r2.controlHash).toBe(r.controlHash);
    expect(r2.arms.map((a) => a.treatedHash)).toEqual(r.arms.map((a) => a.treatedHash));
    expect(r2.envelopes.map((e) => e.envelopeHash)).toEqual(r.envelopes.map((e) => e.envelopeHash));
  }, T);
  it("places the lesion centre on the heaviest cell and the empty control away from life", () => {
    const sim = new RefSim(makeWorld(p, fam));
    sim.run(40);
    const h = heaviestCell(sim.state), e = emptiestCell(sim.state);
    expect(h).not.toEqual(e);
  }, T);
});

describe("observer neutrality bites", () => {
  const p = tinyProtocol();
  const fam = p.families[0];
  const C = p.window.forkStep, H = p.window.horizon;
  it("a perturbing observer on the instrumented history makes runFamily throw", () => {
    expect(() => runFamily(p, fam, undefined, { observer: (s, step, phase) => { if (phase === "history" && step === C) s.cells[0] += 1; } })).toThrow(/observer neutrality failed/);
  }, T);
  it("a perturbing observer on a continuation, only after the fork and at the endpoint, makes runFamily throw (neutrality is checked through C+H)", () => {
    expect(() => runFamily(p, fam, undefined, { observer: (s, step, phase) => { if (phase === "continuation" && step === C + H) s.cells[1] += 1; } })).toThrow(/observer neutrality failed for .*instrumented continuation/);
  }, T);
  it("a harmless observer does not throw", () => {
    let calls = 0;
    runFamily(p, fam, undefined, { observer: () => { calls++; } });
    expect(calls).toBeGreaterThan(10);
  }, T);
  it("checkBooks throws on a nonzero energy or matter residual", () => {
    expect(() => checkBooks({ energyResidual: () => 1n, matterResidual: () => 0n }, "x")).toThrow(/energy ledger residual/);
    expect(() => checkBooks({ energyResidual: () => 0n, matterResidual: () => -3n }, "x")).toThrow(/matter residual/);
    expect(() => checkBooks({ energyResidual: () => 0n, matterResidual: () => 0n }, "x")).not.toThrow();
  });
});

describe("bounds and budget are checked before simulating", () => {
  it("plannedCellSteps counts every arm and twin; out-of-range protocols are refused before any run", () => {
    const p = tinyProtocol();
    const n = p.world.side * p.world.side;
    expect(plannedCellSteps(p)).toBe(n * (2 * p.window.forkStep + 2 * (1 + p.mechanisms.length) * p.window.horizon));
    expect(() => assertFixtureBounds({ ...p, world: { ...p.world, side: 100_000 } })).toThrow(/world.side/);
    expect(() => assertFixtureBounds({ ...p, window: { ...p.window, horizon: 1 } })).toThrow(/window.horizon/);
    expect(() => assertFixtureBounds({ ...p, window: { ...p.window, frameSteps: [p.window.forkStep + 1] } })).toThrow(/frameSteps/);
    expect(() => assertFixtureBounds({ ...p, mechanisms: [{ id: "x", kind: "feed", where: "occupied", r: 99, amount: 5, expected: "up" }] })).toThrow(/mechanism x r/);
  });
  it("generate refuses over the cap before simulating anything (variants included)", () => {
    const p = tinyProtocol();
    p.caps.maxCellStepsPerFamily = 1000;
    expect(() => generate(p, [p.families[0].id], true)).toThrow(/over the cap/);
  });
});

describe("labelOf boundary", () => {
  it("is inclusive at exactly +minEffect and -minEffect, and none just inside", () => {
    expect(labelOf(50, 50)).toBe("up");
    expect(labelOf(-50, 50)).toBe("down");
    expect(labelOf(49, 50)).toBe("none");
    expect(labelOf(-49, 50)).toBe("none");
    expect(labelOf(0, 50)).toBe("none");
    expect(labelOf(51, 50)).toBe("up");
    expect(labelOf(-51, 50)).toBe("down");
  });

describe("trendConfoundOf on a synthetic rising set", () => {
  const row = (mechanismId: string, before: number, treated: number, control: number): CaseRow => ({ caseId: mechanismId, familyId: "f", split: "test", mechanismId, expected: "none", envelopeHash: "h", effect: { bodyMass: treated - control, livingCells: 0, freeNutrient: 0 }, before, treated, control, treatedHash: "t" });
  const rows = [row("sham", 100, 160, 160), row("lowDoseFeed", 100, 163, 160), row("feedOccupied", 100, 260, 160), row("lesionOccupied", 100, 40, 160)];
  it("the naive rule is fooled by the rising world where the paired rule is not", () => {
    const t = trendConfoundOf(rows, 50);
    expect(t.sham).toEqual({ n: 1, naiveUp: 1, pairedNone: 1 });
    expect(t.lowDose).toEqual({ n: 1, naiveUp: 1, pairedNone: 1 });
    expect(t.pairedAlwaysRisingAccuracy).toBe(0.25);
    expect(t.naiveBeforeAfterAlwaysRisingAccuracy).toBe(0.75);
    expect(t.risingCases).toBe(3);
    expect(t.totalCases).toBe(4);
  });
  it("on a declining set the confound cannot show: no rising cases, naive never says up", () => {
    const t = trendConfoundOf([row("sham", 200, 150, 150), row("lowDoseFeed", 200, 153, 150)], 50);
    expect(t.risingCases).toBe(0);
    expect(t.sham.naiveUp).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe("trend-confound truth table", () => {
  // Hand-specified: a rising world (before = 100). Paired effect of sham and a low-dose feed is 0 (none); the naive before/after
  // rule sees treated 160 - before 100 = +60 and calls both "up" at a minimum effect of 50.
  const rows = [
    { name: "sham", before: 100, treated: 160, control: 160, pairedLabel: "none", naiveLabel: "up" },
    { name: "lowDose", before: 100, treated: 163, control: 160, pairedLabel: "none", naiveLabel: "up" },
    { name: "feed", before: 100, treated: 260, control: 160, pairedLabel: "up", naiveLabel: "up" },
    { name: "lesion", before: 100, treated: 40, control: 160, pairedLabel: "down", naiveLabel: "down" },
  ] as const;
  it("matches the hand-derived table", () => {
    for (const r of rows) {
      expect(labelOf(r.treated - r.control, 50), r.name).toBe(r.pairedLabel);
      expect(labelOf(r.treated - r.before, 50), r.name).toBe(r.naiveLabel);
    }
    // alwaysRising ("up" everywhere): right on 2 of 4 by the paired rule, 3 of 4 by the naive rule.
    expect(rows.filter((r) => r.pairedLabel === "up").length).toBe(1);
    expect(rows.filter((r) => r.naiveLabel === "up").length).toBe(3);
  });
});

    expect(g.results).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(g.variants.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(2);
  }, T);
});
// Baseline proposers: validator-clean output, what each one ignores, the shuffled control's donor rules, the v0 adapter and the model seam.
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { bodySha256, canonical, proposalId, RULES, specHash } from "../lib/propose.ts";
import { draftProtocol } from "../lib/prediction/run.ts";
import { abstainAll, alwaysNone, fixedRule, fromRulesProposal, leadProbs, mechanismRule, numericTrend, priorMajority, shuffledEvidence, toPerMille, trendDirection, v0Rules } from "../lib/prediction/proposers.ts";
import { argmax, validatePrediction } from "../lib/prediction/validate.ts";
import { assignSplits, donorsFor } from "../lib/prediction/splits.ts";
import { DRAIN, FEED, LESION, SHAM, censusSeries, synthEnv } from "./prediction-helpers.ts";

const K = draftProtocol().proposers;
const envs = [LESION, FEED, DRAIN, SHAM].map((op, i) => synthEnv(op, { caseId: `c${i}`, census: censusSeries(1000, i - 1.5) }));

describe("proposers", () => {
  it("every baseline emits validator-clean output on every envelope", () => {
    const ps = [fixedRule(K.fixedRule), numericTrend(K.numericTrend), priorMajority({ up: 100, down: 200, none: 700 }), alwaysNone(), abstainAll(), v0Rules(K.v0Rules), mechanismRule(K.mechanismRule)];
    for (const p of ps) for (const e of envs) expect(validatePrediction(p.propose(e), e), `${p.id} on ${e.caseId}`).toEqual({ ok: true, errors: [] });
  });
  it("fixedRule follows the operation and ignores the census", () => {
    const p = fixedRule(K.fixedRule);
    const a = synthEnv(FEED, { census: censusSeries(1000, 5) }), b = synthEnv(FEED, { census: censusSeries(9, -3) });
    expect(p.propose(a).probs).toEqual(p.propose(b).probs);
    expect(p.propose(a).probs).toEqual({ up: 600, down: 200, none: 200 });
    expect(p.propose(synthEnv(LESION)).probs!.down).toBe(600);
    expect(p.propose(synthEnv(DRAIN)).probs!.down).toBe(600);
    expect(p.propose(synthEnv(SHAM)).probs!.none).toBe(600);
  });
  it("numericTrend ignores frames and the task", () => {
    const p = numericTrend(K.numericTrend);
    const a = synthEnv(FEED, { census: censusSeries(1000, 2) });
    const b = { ...synthEnv(LESION, { census: censusSeries(1000, 2) }), frames: [] };
    expect(p.propose(a).probs).toEqual(p.propose(b as never).probs);
    expect(trendDirection(censusSeries(1000, 2), 200, K.numericTrend)).toBe("up");
    expect(trendDirection(censusSeries(1000, -2), 200, K.numericTrend)).toBe("down");
    expect(trendDirection(censusSeries(1000, 0), 200, K.numericTrend)).toBe("none");
  });
  it("alwaysNone and priorMajority are valid forecasts; abstainAll abstains", () => {
    const e = envs[0];
    expect(alwaysNone().propose(e).probs).toEqual({ up: 0, down: 0, none: 1000 });
    expect(priorMajority({ up: 139, down: 208, none: 653 }).propose(e).probs).toEqual({ up: 139, down: 208, none: 653 });
    expect(abstainAll().propose(e).abstain).toBe(true);
  });
  it("leadProbs and toPerMille always sum to 1000", () => {
    for (const lead of [334, 500, 601, 999]) for (const d of ["up", "down", "none"] as const) {
      const p = leadProbs(d, lead);
      expect(p.up + p.down + p.none).toBe(1000);
      expect(p[d]).toBe(lead);
    }
    const q = toPerMille({ up: 1, down: 1, none: 1 });
    expect(q.up + q.down + q.none).toBe(1000);
  });
  it("the v0 rule proposer maps through fromRulesProposal (direction mass, remainder on none)", () => {
    const e = synthEnv(FEED);
    expect(v0Rules(K.v0Rules).propose(e).probs).toEqual({ up: 600, down: 0, none: 400 });
    expect(v0Rules(K.v0Rules).propose(synthEnv(SHAM)).probs).toEqual({ up: 0, down: 0, none: 1000 });
    expect(fromRulesProposal(e, { kind: "directional", statement: "s", direction: RULES.livingCells.high.direction }, 600).probs).toEqual({ up: 0, down: 600, none: 400 });
  });
  it("hashes of existing v0 helpers are unchanged by the additive export (values computed from the HEAD copy of propose.ts)", () => {
    expect(canonical({ b: 1, a: [2, { d: 1, c: 2 }] })).toBe('{"a":[2,{"c":2,"d":1}],"b":1}');
    const body = { proposalVersion: 0, generator: "rules-v0", constants: { W: 50, burnInStep: 10000, horizon: 20000, threshold: 1, feedAmount: 64 }, ring: { index: 3, signal: "livingCells", label: "x", side: "low", D: 2.5, spread: 0.5 }, rule: { action: "feed", hypothesis: "h" }, fork: { step: 1000, check: "initHash", hash: "ab" }, interventionStep: 2000, replayThrough: 2000, argv: ["a", "b"], reason: "r", prediction: { kind: "directional", statement: "s", direction: "up" } } as never;
    expect(bodySha256(body)).toBe("b8f45c64a8b4264807ce939656d9db86f5431401b3b425801c18b05719745a0f");
    expect(proposalId(body)).toBe("b8f45c64a8b42648");
    const spec = { experiment: "naturalist-fork", presetId: "archipelago", condition: "neutral", seed: 1, steps: 100, censusEvery: 10 } as never;
    expect(specHash(spec)).toBe("9f1b5c26fb19ab465e92b9ff5a5a004fe9bd1606b7fd5a5463f18e83b35ee00f");
  });
});

describe("shuffledEvidence", () => {
  const plan = assignSplits(["fA", "fB", "fC", "fD", "fE", "fF"], "salt", 3, 1);
  const donors = donorsFor(plan, "salt");
  const byFam = new Map(plan.dev.concat(plan.test).map((f, i) => [f, synthEnv(FEED, { familyId: f, caseId: `d-${f}`, census: censusSeries(1000, i - 2) })]));
  const splitOf = (f: string) => (plan.dev.includes(f) ? "dev" : plan.test.includes(f) ? "test" : undefined) as "dev" | "test" | undefined;
  it("is deterministic, uses a different family of the same split, and equals numericTrend on the donor's census", () => {
    const p = shuffledEvidence(K.numericTrend, donors, (f) => byFam.get(f), splitOf);
    for (const [f, e] of byFam) {
      const d = donors[f];
      expect(d).not.toBe(f);
      expect(splitOf(d)).toBe(splitOf(f));
      expect(p.propose(e)).toEqual(p.propose(e));
      expect(p.propose(e).probs).toEqual(numericTrend(K.numericTrend).propose(byFam.get(d)!).probs);
    }
  });
  it("refuses a later window of the same history, and a donor from another split", () => {
    const own = synthEnv(FEED, { familyId: "fA", historyId: "fA:s1", caseId: "own" });
    const later = synthEnv(FEED, { familyId: "fZ", historyId: "fA:s1", caseId: "later" });
    const p = shuffledEvidence(K.numericTrend, { fA: "fZ" }, () => later, () => "dev");
    expect(() => p.propose(own)).toThrow(/same history/);
    const cross = shuffledEvidence(K.numericTrend, { fA: "fB" }, () => synthEnv(FEED, { familyId: "fB", caseId: "x" }), (f) => (f === "fA" ? "dev" : "test"));
    expect(() => cross.propose(own)).toThrow(/another split/);
    const same = shuffledEvidence(K.numericTrend, { fA: "fA" }, () => own, () => "dev");
    expect(() => same.propose(own)).toThrow(/same family/);
  });
});

describe("numericTrend vs shuffledEvidence on opposite trends", () => {
  // The delivered real run has every fixture world declining, so both predict "down" everywhere; this proves the harness CAN separate them.
  const rising = synthEnv(FEED, { familyId: "fR", caseId: "rise", census: censusSeries(1000, 2) });
  const falling = synthEnv(FEED, { familyId: "fF", caseId: "fall", census: censusSeries(1000, -2) });
  it("numericTrend says up on a rising census and down on a falling one", () => {
    expect(argmax(numericTrend(K.numericTrend).propose(rising).probs!)).toBe("up");
    expect(argmax(numericTrend(K.numericTrend).propose(falling).probs!)).toBe("down");
  });
  it("shuffledEvidence differs from numericTrend on at least one such pair, because it reads the donor's census", () => {
    const sh = shuffledEvidence(K.numericTrend, { fR: "fF", fF: "fR" }, (f) => (f === "fR" ? rising : falling), () => "test");
    const nt = numericTrend(K.numericTrend);
    expect(argmax(sh.propose(rising).probs!)).toBe("down");
    expect(argmax(sh.propose(falling).probs!)).toBe("up");
    const differs = [rising, falling].filter((e) => canonical(sh.propose(e).probs) !== canonical(nt.propose(e).probs));
    expect(differs.length).toBe(2);
  });
});

describe("module hygiene", () => {
  it("the model seam and the proposers import nothing outside the repo and no network API", () => {
    for (const f of ["model-seam.ts", "proposers.ts", "validate.ts", "score.ts"]) {
      const src = readFileSync(new URL(`../lib/prediction/${f}`, import.meta.url), "utf8");
      const mods = [...src.matchAll(/from\s+"([^"]+)"/g)].map((m) => m[1]);
      for (const m of mods) expect(m.startsWith(".") || m.startsWith("@bl/"), `${f} imports ${m}`).toBe(true);
      expect(src).not.toMatch(/\bfetch\s*\(|XMLHttpRequest|WebSocket|node:http|node:net/);
    }
  });
});

1. **P1 — Confirmation does not require a committed freeze.** [tools/predict.ts:91](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i02-prediction-envelope/tools/predict.ts:91)  
   A matching working-tree protocol/hash pair passes confirmation even when neither file exists in HEAD. The supplied artifacts already have `status: CONFIRM` under that condition, violating PLAN §7’s commit-before-test boundary. **Fix:** verify the protocol and freeze against committed blobs before generating test outcomes. Treat existing inspected families as exploratory when establishing fresh confirmation evidence.

2. **P2 — An empty predictions file passes validation.** [tools/predict.ts:139](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i02-prediction-envelope/tools/predict.ts:139)  
   Empty input creates no groups, skips validation, and returns success. Reproduced: validating `/dev/null` against the supplied **144 envelopes exits 0**. **Fix:** validate an empty submission against the expected cases and report all missing IDs.

3. **P2 — The raw-object adapter accepts predictions that lose required fields when serialized.** [validate.ts:79](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i02-prediction-envelope/tools/lib/prediction/validate.ts:79)  
   `Object.create(goodPrediction)` validates successfully but serializes as `{}`. Likewise, inherited probability fields pass despite serializing as `"probs":{}`. **Fix:** require JSON-compatible records with required fields present as own enumerable properties; add serialization-stability rejection tests.

4. **P2 — Scoring discards file-level invalid/extra diagnostics.** [run.ts:248](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i02-prediction-envelope/tools/lib/prediction/run.ts:248)  
   Supplying 144 valid forecasts plus an unknown case produces `FileCheck.invalid=1` and an extra ID, but the headline score reports `invalid=0`, `missing=0`, `answered=144`. **Fix:** propagate file-level diagnostics into the comparison separately from expected-case scoring.

5. **P2 — The primary endpoint differs from PLAN.md.** [readouts.ts:9](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i02-prediction-envelope/tools/lib/prediction/readouts.ts:9)  
   PLAN specifies `livingCells`; implementation and outputs use `bodyMass`. This changes labels materially: supplied feed cases include zero living-cell effect but positive mass effects labelled `up`. The explanatory comment cites an absent EVIDENCE.md. **Fix:** explicitly amend/version the specification and record qualification evidence, or implement the specified endpoint.

6. **P2 — Qualification uses all development families instead of the reserved subset.** [tools/predict.ts:85](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i02-prediction-envelope/tools/predict.ts:85)  
   The qualification file exposes effects and threshold tallies for **12 families**, although `qualify` contains four. This breaks PLAN’s restriction on fixture and threshold tuning. **Fix:** restrict qualification/tuning inputs to `plan.qualify`; calculate the full dev prior separately.

7. **P2 — The “dev-only” mechanism oracle runs on test cases.** [run.ts:242](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i02-prediction-envelope/tools/lib/prediction/run.ts:242)  
   Every proposer runs on every envelope. Artifacts contain **72 test oracle predictions**, displayed and correctness-coloured in the notebook. Only its test summary row is excluded. **Fix:** restrict oracle execution, serialization, and presentation to development cases.

8. **P2 — Observer-neutrality verification stops at C.** [fixtures.ts:146](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i02-prediction-envelope/tools/lib/prediction/fixtures.ts:146)  
   Instrumented/plain comparisons end at the fork. Scheduled continuations run once, so `neutralityChecks` does not establish PLAN’s required equality through `C+H`. **Fix:** compare independent instrumented/plain continuations with identical schedules through the endpoint, including their execution cost.

9. **P2 — The claimed source-closure hash omits executable dependencies.** [tools/predict.ts:51](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i02-prediction-envelope/tools/predict.ts:51)  
   Changes to `world.ts`, `accounting.ts`, presets, genome/kernel/RNG helpers, or the shared report helper can change results without changing this selected-file hash. **Fix:** hash the transitive local source closure or conservatively hash all relevant package/tool sources.

10. **P2 — Execution limits are checked after spending the work.** [run.ts:106](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i02-prediction-envelope/tools/lib/prediction/run.ts:106)  
    Increasing the horizon beyond the cell-step budget runs the entire simulation before rejection; variant runs bypass this check. **Fix:** validate integer bounds and calculate planned cell-steps before simulation, including variants.

11. **P2 — The advertised `report` command always fails.** [tools/predict.ts:161](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i02-prediction-envelope/tools/predict.ts:161)  
    `predict.ts report --out …` unconditionally exits 1 and directs users to rerun confirmation. **Fix:** render from existing canonical inputs without generating outcomes again, then refresh the manifest.

12. **P3 — Stored canonical artifacts are stale against the renderer.** [report-html.ts:102](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i02-prediction-envelope/tools/lib/prediction/report-html.ts:102)  
    Full in-memory regeneration reproduces seven canonical files, but changes `report.html` and `canonical.sha256`. The stored report lacks `code{overflow-wrap:anywhere}`. Metadata also records an older source hash. **Fix:** regenerate the report, manifest, and metadata from the final source.

13. **P3 — The proposal-hash regression test never computes a proposal hash.** [prediction-proposers.test.ts:58](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i02-prediction-envelope/tools/test/prediction-proposers.test.ts:58)  
    The test checks only a canonical JSON string. A regression in `bodySha256` or `proposalId` would leave it green. **Fix:** pin representative existing proposal hashes/IDs to independently recorded values.

- **Determinism:** finding 12 is artifact drift. No key-order, Map/Set, float, clock, hardware, or metadata-exclusion nondeterminism found.

// The host validator (docs/prediction-envelope-2026-11-01/PLAN.md, section 3): what it accepts, what it rejects, and that a sham is no operation.
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { validateInterventions } from "@bl/runner";
import { sealEnvelope, envelopeBody } from "../lib/prediction/envelope.ts";
import { adaptModelOutput, REGISTERED_ADAPTERS } from "../lib/prediction/model-seam.ts";
import { argmax, taskSchedule, validateEnvelope, validatePrediction, validatePredictionFile, type Prediction } from "../lib/prediction/validate.ts";
import { DRAIN, FEED, LESION, SHAM, synthEnv } from "./prediction-helpers.ts";

const good = (env = synthEnv(LESION)): Prediction => ({ version: 1, caseId: env.caseId, envelopeHash: env.envelopeHash, task: env.task, probs: { up: 100, down: 700, none: 200 }, abstain: false, reason: "r" });

describe("serialization stability", () => {
  const env = synthEnv(LESION);
  it("rejects an object whose required fields are inherited (it would serialize as {})", () => {
    const inherited = Object.create(good(env));
    expect(validatePrediction(inherited, env).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(JSON.stringify(inherited)).toBe("{}");
    const bareProto = Object.create(null);
    Object.assign(bareProto, good(env));
    expect(validatePrediction(bareProto, env).ok).toBe(true); // a null-prototype record is plain JSON data
  });
  it("rejects inherited probs, an inherited task, and a toJSON that changes what is stored", () => {
    const g = good(env);
    expect(validatePrediction({ ...g, probs: Object.create(g.probs!) }, env).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(validatePrediction({ ...g, task: Object.create(g.task) }, env).ok).toBe(false);
    const sneaky = { ...g, probs: { ...g.probs!, toJSON: () => ({ up: 0, down: 0, none: 1000 }) } };
    expect(validatePrediction(sneaky, env).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(validatePrediction({ ...g, reason: undefined }, env).ok).toBe(false);
  });
  it("a prediction that validates survives a JSON round trip unchanged", () => {
    const g = good(env);
    expect(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(g))).toEqual(g);
  });
  it("validatePredictionFile counts an unsound envelope (stale hash) as invalid and lists it", () => {
    const tampered = { ...env, census: env.census.map((c, i) => (i === 0 ? { ...c, mass: c.mass + 1 } : c)) };
    const r = validatePredictionFile([JSON.stringify(good(env))], [tampered]);
    expect(r.badEnvelopes).toEqual([env.caseId]);
    expect(r.invalid).toBe(1);
    expect(r.byCase.get(env.caseId)!.prediction).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("validatePrediction", () => {
  it("accepts menu lesion, feed, drain and sham tasks", () => {
    for (const op of [LESION, FEED, DRAIN, SHAM]) {
      const env = synthEnv(op);
      expect(validatePrediction(good(env), env)).toEqual({ ok: true, errors: [] });
    }
  });
  it("accepts an abstention without probs", () => {
    const env = synthEnv(LESION);
    const { probs: _p, ...rest } = good(env);
    void _p;
    expect(validatePrediction({ ...rest, abstain: true }, env).ok).toBe(true);
  });
  it.each([
    ["sum 999", { up: 100, down: 700, none: 199 }],
    ["sum 1001", { up: 100, down: 700, none: 201 }],
    ["float", { up: 100.5, down: 699.5, none: 200 }],
    ["negative", { up: -100, down: 900, none: 200 }],
    ["over 1000", { up: 0, down: 1001, none: -1 }],
    ["extra class", { up: 0, down: 500, none: 500, maybe: 0 }],
    ["missing class", { up: 500, down: 500 }],
  ])("rejects probs with %s", (_n, probs) => {
    const env = synthEnv(LESION);
    expect(validatePrediction({ ...good(env), probs }, env).ok).toBe(false);
  });
  it("rejects an abstention that carries probs, and missing probs without abstaining", () => {
    const env = synthEnv(LESION);
    expect(validatePrediction({ ...good(env), abstain: true }, env).ok).toBe(false);
    const { probs: _p, ...rest } = good(env);
    void _p;
    expect(validatePrediction({ ...rest, abstain: false }, env).ok).toBe(false);
  });
  it("rejects a task that differs in readout, intervention or time (the question is fixed)", () => {
    const env = synthEnv(LESION);
    expect(validatePrediction({ ...good(env), task: { ...env.task, readout: "livingCells" } }, env).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(validatePrediction({ ...good(env), task: { ...env.task, op: { kind: "none" } } }, env).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(validatePrediction({ ...good(env), task: { ...env.task, delta: 11 } }, env).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(validatePrediction({ ...good(env), task: { ...env.task, horizon: 100 } }, env).ok).toBe(false);
  });
  it("rejects extra keys, wrong caseId or hash, __proto__ keys and non-objects", () => {
    const env = synthEnv(LESION);
    expect(validatePrediction({ ...good(env), extra: 1 }, env).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(validatePrediction({ ...good(env), caseId: "other" }, env).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(validatePrediction({ ...good(env), envelopeHash: "0".repeat(64) }, env).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(validatePrediction(JSON.parse(`{"__proto__":{"x":1},"caseId":"${env.caseId}"}`), env).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(validatePrediction(JSON.parse(`{"constructor":1}`), env).ok).toBe(false);
    for (const bad of [null, 3, "x", [1]]) expect(validatePrediction(bad, env).ok).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("tasks and the menu", () => {
  it("converts every non-sham operation to a schedule the runner validator accepts", () => {
    for (const op of [LESION, FEED, DRAIN]) {
      const t = taskSchedule(synthEnv(op));
      expect(t.errors).toEqual([]);
      expect(t.schedule).not.toBeNull();
      expect(validateInterventions(t.schedule)).toEqual([]);
      expect(t.schedule![0].step).toBe(310);
    }
    expect(taskSchedule(synthEnv(DRAIN)).schedule![0]).toMatchObject({ kind: "feed", amount: -64 });
  });
  it("maps the sham to no scheduled operation", () => {
    expect(taskSchedule(synthEnv(SHAM))).toEqual({ schedule: null, errors: [] });
  });
  it("does not offer a radius-0 lesion or a zero feed", () => {
    expect(taskSchedule(synthEnv({ kind: "lesion", x: 1, y: 1, r: 0 })).errors.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(taskSchedule(synthEnv({ kind: "feed", x: 1, y: 1, r: 2, amount: 0 })).errors.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
  it("rejects out-of-menu operations, radii, amounts, times and centres", () => {
    expect(taskSchedule(synthEnv({ kind: "sun" as never })).errors.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(taskSchedule(synthEnv({ kind: "lesion", x: 1, y: 1, r: 7 })).errors.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(taskSchedule(synthEnv({ kind: "feed", x: 1, y: 1, r: 2, amount: 4097 })).errors.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(taskSchedule(synthEnv({ kind: "lesion", x: 99, y: 1, r: 2 })).errors.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(taskSchedule(synthEnv(LESION, { delta: 0 })).errors.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(taskSchedule(synthEnv(LESION, { delta: 400 })).errors.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(taskSchedule(synthEnv({ kind: "none", r: 3 })).errors.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(taskSchedule(synthEnv({ kind: "lesion", x: 1, y: 1, r: 2, amount: 5 })).errors.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
  it("detects a tampered envelope", () => {
    const env = synthEnv(LESION);
    expect(validateEnvelope(env).ok).toBe(true);
    expect(validateEnvelope({ ...env, census: [...env.census.slice(1)] }).ok).toBe(false);
    void sealEnvelope;
    void envelopeBody;
  });
});

describe("argmax tie order", () => {
  it("breaks ties none > down > up", () => {
    expect(argmax({ up: 500, down: 500, none: 0 })).toBe("down");
    expect(argmax({ up: 334, down: 333, none: 333 })).toBe("up");
    expect(argmax({ up: 333, down: 333, none: 334 })).toBe("none");
    expect(argmax({ up: 0, down: 500, none: 500 })).toBe("none");
  });
});

describe("file-level checks and the model seam", () => {
  it("counts duplicate, missing and extra cases, and never repairs", () => {
    const a = synthEnv(LESION, { caseId: "cA" }), b = synthEnv(FEED, { caseId: "cB" });
    const lines = [JSON.stringify(good(a)), JSON.stringify(good(a)), JSON.stringify({ ...good(a), caseId: "zzz" }), "not json", "[1]"];
    const r = validatePredictionFile(lines, [a, b]);
    expect(r.duplicates).toEqual(["cA"]);
    expect(r.missing).toEqual(["cB"]);
    expect(r.extra).toEqual(["zzz"]);
    expect(r.notObject).toBe(2);
    expect(r.byCase.get("cA")!.prediction).toBeNull();
  });
  it("routes raw model output through the validator, rejecting out-of-menu output", () => {
    const env = synthEnv(LESION);
    expect(adaptModelOutput(JSON.stringify(good(env)), env).prediction).not.toBeNull();
    const other = { ...good(env), task: { ...env.task, op: { kind: "feed", x: 1, y: 1, r: 9, amount: 5 } } };
    expect(adaptModelOutput(other, env).prediction).toBeNull();
    expect(adaptModelOutput("{broken", env).validation.ok).toBe(false);
  });
  it("registers no adapter by default", () => {
    expect(REGISTERED_ADAPTERS).toHaveLength(0);
  });
});
